## Level 1 – English 7: Fruit Salad

Topic: Food and cooking. About 73 words, 13 sentences.

### Word cards (4)

**peel** – To take the skin off fruit or vegetables. e.g. Gran peels the potatoes. I peel, I peeled. Afrikaans: "skil". *Picture: a hand peeling a banana.*

**slice** – A thin, flat piece cut from something. e.g. Can I have a slice of bread? One slice, many slices. Afrikaans: "skyf". *Picture: banana slices.*

**juicy** – Full of juice. e.g. The watermelon is sweet and juicy. Afrikaans: "sappig". *Picture: a juicy orange.*

**chop** – To cut something into small pieces. e.g. Dad chops the onions. I chop, I chopped. Afrikaans: "opkap". *Picture: chopped apple on a board.*

### The passage

Today we make a fruit salad.\
Mom washes the fruit in the sink.\
I peel two yellow bananas.\
Mom cuts them into slices.\
I pull the grapes off the stem.\
We cut a juicy orange.\
The juice runs down my arm!\
Mom chops a green apple.\
I add some red strawberries.\
We mix all the fruit in a bowl.\
Then we add a spoon of yoghurt.\
The salad is fresh and sweet.\
Yum!

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **Where does Mom wash the fruit?** **In the sink. ✓** / In the bath. / In a bucket. / In the pool. / In the river.
2. **How many bananas do I peel?** One. / Three. / **Two. ✓** / Four. / Five.
3. **What runs down my arm?** Water. / **The juice. ✓** / Yoghurt. / Rain. / Milk.
4. **What colour is the apple?** Red. / Yellow. / Orange. / **Green. ✓** / Purple.
5. **What do we add at the end?** Sugar. / Ice cream. / Honey. / Cream. / **A spoon of yoghurt. ✓**

### Word recognition (10)

fruit, salad, wash, peel, bananas, grapes, orange, juicy, apple, bowl

### Grammar: plurals (5)

*Give the plural – e.g. cat – cats*

1. banana – **bananas**
2. grape – **grapes**
3. peach – **peaches**
4. strawberry – **strawberries**
5. cherry – **cherries**

### Vocabulary (5)

1. Please \_\_\_\_\_\_ the orange before you eat it. pool / **peel ✓** / peal / pill
2. Can I have a \_\_\_\_\_\_ of cake? slide / slices / **slice ✓** / slip
3. The watermelon is sweet and \_\_\_\_\_\_. **juicy ✓** / juice / juices / jolly
4. Dad \_\_\_\_\_\_ the onions into small pieces. chop / chips / shops / **chops ✓**
5. We mix the fruit in a big \_\_\_\_\_\_. ball / **bowl ✓** / bell / bowls

## Level 1 – English 8: The Treasure Map

Topic: Adventure and make-believe. About 79 words, 13 sentences.

### Word cards (4)

**treasure** – Gold, jewels or other precious things, often hidden. e.g. The pirates hid their treasure on an island. Afrikaans: "skat". *Picture: a treasure chest.*

**follow** – To go after someone or something. e.g. The ducklings follow their mother. I follow, I followed. Afrikaans: "volg". *Picture: ducklings behind a duck.*

**bridge** – Something built over a river or road so you can cross it. e.g. The car drives over the bridge. One bridge, many bridges. Afrikaans: "brug". *Picture: a small wooden bridge.*

**coin** – A small, round piece of metal money. e.g. I put a coin in my piggy bank. One coin, many coins. Afrikaans: "muntstuk". *Picture: gold coins.*

### The passage

I find an old map in Grandpa's shed.\
It is a treasure map!\
There is a big red X on it.\
My brother and I follow the map.\
We walk past the big oak tree.\
We cross the little wooden bridge.\
We count ten steps to the rock.\
Then we start to dig.\
Clunk! My spade hits a box.\
We open the lid slowly.\
Inside there are shiny gold coins!\
They are made of chocolate.\
What a sweet surprise!

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **Where do I find the map?** Under my bed. / **In Grandpa's shed. ✓** / In a book. / At school. / In the garden.
2. **What is on the map?** **A big red X. ✓** / A blue circle. / A green star. / A black dog. / A yellow sun.
3. **What do we cross?** A big river. / The road. / **The little wooden bridge. ✓** / A fence. / A wall.
4. **What does my spade hit?** A rock. / A bone. / A pipe. / **A box. ✓** / A shoe.
5. **What are the coins made of?** Gold. / Silver. / Plastic. / Paper. / **Chocolate. ✓**

### Word recognition (10)

map, treasure, follow, tree, bridge, steps, rock, spade, coins, chocolate

### Grammar: a or an (5)

*Write a or an – e.g. an apple, a dog*

1. \_\_\_ map – **a**
2. \_\_\_ old box – **an**
3. \_\_\_ egg – **an**
4. \_\_\_ bridge – **a**
5. \_\_\_ island – **an**

### Vocabulary (5)

1. The pirates buried their \_\_\_\_\_\_ on the island. measure / **treasure ✓** / pleasure / teacher
2. The ducklings \_\_\_\_\_\_ their mother. **follow ✓** / fellow / hollow / follows
3. We walk over the \_\_\_\_\_\_ to cross the river. fridge / bride / **bridge ✓** / badge
4. I put a \_\_\_\_\_\_ in my piggy bank. corn / coins / cone / **coin ✓**
5. We use a \_\_\_\_\_\_ to find the way. mop / **map ✓** / nap / maps

## Level 1 – English 9: The Big Tree

Topic: Nature and outdoors. About 80 words, 12 sentences.

### Word cards (4)

**trunk** – The thick main stem of a tree. e.g. The tree trunk is too wide to hug. One trunk, many trunks. Afrikaans: "stam". *Picture: a thick tree trunk.*

**branch** – A part of a tree that grows out from the trunk. e.g. The monkey sits on a branch. One branch, many branches. Afrikaans: "tak". *Picture: a bird on a branch.*

**nest** – A home that birds build from sticks and grass. e.g. There are three eggs in the nest. One nest, many nests. Afrikaans: "nes". *Picture: a nest with eggs.*

**shade** – A cool, dark place where the sun does not shine. e.g. The dog lies in the shade. Afrikaans: "koelte". *Picture: a child resting under a tree.*

### The passage

There is a big tree in our garden.\
It is very old and tall.\
Its trunk is thick and brown.\
Its leaves are green in summer.\
In autumn they turn yellow and fall.\
Birds build nests in the branches.\
A squirrel runs up and down the trunk.\
I sit in the shade on hot days.\
Dad hangs a swing from a strong branch.\
I swing high into the air.\
The tree gives us fresh air.\
I love our big tree.

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **What colour is the trunk?** Grey. / Black. / **Brown. ✓** / Green. / White.
2. **What happens to the leaves in autumn?** They grow bigger. / **They turn yellow and fall. ✓** / They turn blue. / They turn into flowers. / Nothing happens.
3. **What do birds build in the branches?** Houses. / Bridges. / Swings. / **Nests. ✓** / Webs.
4. **Where do I sit on hot days?** **In the shade. ✓** / In the sun. / In the pool. / In the house. / In the car.
5. **What does Dad hang from a branch?** A lamp. / A bird house. / A flag. / A rope ladder. / **A swing. ✓**

### Word recognition (10)

garden, tall, trunk, thick, leaves, autumn, nests, branches, shade, swing

### Grammar: plurals (5)

*Give the plural – e.g. cat – cats*

1. tree – **trees**
2. nest – **nests**
3. branch – **branches**
4. bush – **bushes**
5. leaf – **leaves**

### Vocabulary (5)

1. The \_\_\_\_\_\_ of the tree is thick and brown. truck / **trunk ✓** / trick / trunks
2. The monkey sits on a high \_\_\_\_\_\_. brunch / bench / **branch ✓** / branches
3. The bird lays eggs in its \_\_\_\_\_\_. **nest ✓** / next / net / vest
4. It is hot, so let's sit in the \_\_\_\_\_\_. shape / shake / spade / **shade ✓**
5. I go high on my \_\_\_\_\_\_. sing / **swing ✓** / wing / string

## Level 1 – English 10: My Cat Coco

Topic: Animals. About 76 words, 11 sentences.

### Word cards (4)

**paw** – The soft foot of an animal like a cat or dog. e.g. The puppy gave me its paw. One paw, many paws. Afrikaans: "poot". *Picture: a cat's paw.*

**whiskers** – The long, stiff hairs on a cat's face. e.g. The mouse's whiskers twitch. Afrikaans: "snorhare". *Picture: a cat's face with whiskers.*

**purr** – The soft, happy sound a cat makes. e.g. The kitten purrs on my lap. It purrs, it purred. Afrikaans: "spin". *Picture: a happy cat on a lap.*

**stroke** – To move your hand gently over something. e.g. I stroke the horse's nose. I stroke, I stroked. Afrikaans: "streel". *Picture: a hand stroking a cat.*

### The passage

I have a cat called Coco.\
She is black with white paws.\
She has long whiskers and green eyes.\
Coco likes to sleep in the sun.\
She purrs when I stroke her soft fur.\
In the morning she eats fish from her bowl.\
Coco loves to play with a ball of wool.\
She chases it under the couch.\
At night she climbs onto my bed.\
She curls up by my feet.\
Coco is my best friend.

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **What colour are Coco's paws?** Black. / **White. ✓** / Brown. / Grey. / Orange.
2. **What colour are her eyes?** Blue. / Brown. / **Green. ✓** / Yellow. / Black.
3. **When does Coco purr?** When she eats. / When she is angry. / When it rains. / **When I stroke her soft fur. ✓** / When the dog barks.
4. **What does Coco eat in the morning?** **Fish. ✓** / Meat. / Bread. / Milk. / Cheese.
5. **Where does she curl up at night?** On the couch. / In her box. / Under the table. / On the roof. / **By my feet. ✓**

### Word recognition (10)

cat, black, paws, whiskers, green, purrs, stroke, fur, chases, curls

### Grammar: adding -ing (5)

*Add -ing – e.g. jump – jumping*

1. sleep – **sleeping**
2. purr – **purring**
3. chase – **chasing**
4. climb – **climbing**
5. curl – **curling**

### Vocabulary (5)

1. The dog gave me its \_\_\_\_\_\_. pay / **paw ✓** / saw / pow
2. My cat has long white \_\_\_\_\_\_. **whiskers ✓** / whispers / whisker / whisks
3. The kitten \_\_\_\_\_\_ on my lap. pours / purse / **purrs ✓** / purr
4. I \_\_\_\_\_\_ my cat's soft fur. strike / smoke / strokes / **stroke ✓**
5. The cat \_\_\_\_\_\_ the mouse around the house. chase / **chases ✓** / cheeses / cases

## Level 2 – Afrikaans: Oupa se skilpad

About 112 words, 14 sentences averaging 8 words, one sentence per line, a simple story. Font: Andika. Word cards get a picture.

### Woordkaarte (5)

**skilpad** – 'n Dier met 'n harde dop op sy rug. Hy loop baie stadig. Bv. Die skilpad trek sy kop in sy dop in. Een skilpad, baie skilpaaie. Engels: "tortoise". *Prent: 'n skilpad in die gras.*

**nêrens** – Op geen plek nie. Bv. Ek kan my skoen nêrens kry nie. Teenoorgestelde: oral. Engels: "nowhere". *Prent: 'n kind wat oral soek.*

**kruiwa** – 'n Karretjie met een wiel waarmee jy grond of klippe stoot. Bv. Pa stoot die sand met die kruiwa. Een kruiwa, baie kruiwaens. Engels: "wheelbarrow". *Prent: 'n kruiwa vol grond.*

**loer** – Om vinnig of stil na iets te kyk. Bv. Die kat loer by die venster in. Ek loer, ek het geloer. Engels: "peep". *Prent: 'n kind wat agter 'n deur loer.*

**stout** – Wanneer jy iets doen wat jy nie mag doen nie. Bv. Die stout hond het my skoen gesteel. Teenoorgestelde: soet. Engels: "naughty". *Prent: 'n hond met 'n skoen in sy bek.*

### Die leesstuk

Oupa het 'n ou skilpad in sy tuin.\
Sy naam is Stadig, want hy loop baie stadig.\
Stadig eet graag blare, blomme en rooi tamaties.\
Een oggend kan ons hom nêrens kry nie.\
Ek soek onder die bosse en agter die hok.\
My sussie kyk tussen die groot klippe.\
Oupa loer selfs in die ou kruiwa.\
Ons soek en soek, maar Stadig is weg.\
Toe hoor ek 'n geluid by die groentebedding.\
Daar sit Stadig tussen Ouma se tamaties!\
Hy het al drie tamaties opgeëet.\
Ouma lag en sê hy is 'n stout skilpad.\
Nou sit Oupa 'n heining om die groente.\
Stadig kan nie meer by die tamaties kom nie.

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Wat is die skilpad se naam?** Sy naam is Vinnig. / **Sy naam is Stadig. ✓** / Sy naam is Dikkop. / Sy naam is Stoutjie. / Sy naam is Kleintjie.
2. **Waar soek die sussie?** Sy soek onder die bosse. / Sy soek in die kruiwa. / **Sy soek tussen die groot klippe. ✓** / Sy soek agter die hok. / Sy soek in die huis.
3. **Waar kry hulle vir Stadig?** Hulle kry hom by die hok. / Hulle kry hom by die kraan. / Hulle kry hom onder die bed. / **Hulle kry hom by die groentebedding. ✓** / Hulle kry hom in die kruiwa.
4. **Hoeveel tamaties het Stadig geëet?** Hy het een tamatie geëet. / Hy het twee tamaties geëet. / Hy het vier tamaties geëet. / Hy het vyf tamaties geëet. / **Hy het drie tamaties geëet. ✓**
5. **Wat doen Oupa aan die einde?** **Hy sit 'n heining om die groente. ✓** / Hy koop nuwe tamaties. / Hy bou 'n hok vir Stadig. / Hy gee Stadig weg. / Hy plant nog blomme.

### Woordherkenning (10)

skilpad, stadig, blare, tamaties, bosse, klippe, kruiwa, nêrens, stout, heining

### Grammatika: teenoorgesteldes (5)

*Gee die teenoorgestelde – Bv. mooi – lelik*

1. stadig – **vinnig**
2. groot – **klein**
3. stout – **soet**
4. lag – **huil**
5. ou – **nuwe** *(accept: nuut, jonk)*

### Woordeskat (5)

1. Die \_\_\_\_\_\_ trek sy kop in sy harde dop in. **skilpad ✓** / skilpaaie / skulp / skip
2. Ek kan my potlood \_\_\_\_\_\_ kry nie. nerens / êrens / **nêrens ✓** / nêrense
3. Pa stoot die klippe met die \_\_\_\_\_\_. kruie / **kruiwa ✓** / kruiwaens / kruis
4. Die kat \_\_\_\_\_\_ deur die venster na die voëls. loor / leer / loere / **loer ✓**
5. Die \_\_\_\_\_\_ hondjie het Ma se skoen gesteel. stoot / **stout ✓** / stoep / sout

## Level 2 – Afrikaans 2: Die groot wedloop

Topic: Sport and games. About 115 words, 14 sentences.

### Woordkaarte (5)

**wedloop** – 'n Resies waar mense hardloop om te sien wie die vinnigste is. Bv. Die haas en die skilpad het 'n wedloop gehou. Een wedloop, baie wedlope. Engels: "race". *Prent: kinders wat hardloop.*

**pawiljoen** – Die rye sitplekke waar mense sit om sport te kyk. Bv. Ouma sit op die pawiljoen en kyk. Een pawiljoen, baie pawiljoene. Engels: "grandstand". *Prent: mense op 'n pawiljoen.*

**lint** – 'n Lang, smal strook stof of papier. Bv. Ma knoop 'n rooi lint in my hare. Een lint, baie linte. Engels: "ribbon / tape". *Prent: 'n atleet wat deur die lint breek.*

**medalje** – 'n Ronde stuk metaal wat jy kry as jy iets wen. Bv. Die wenner kry 'n goue medalje. Een medalje, baie medaljes. Engels: "medal". *Prent: 'n goue medalje.*

**skaduwee** – 'n Koel, donker plek waar die son nie skyn nie. Bv. Die hond lê in die skaduwee. Engels: "shade". *Prent: kinders onder 'n boom.*

### Die leesstuk

Dit is sportdag by ons skool.\
Almal dra ons skool se kleure, blou en wit.\
Ek doen mee aan die honderd meter wedloop.\
My hart klop vinnig voor die begin.\
Die onderwyser blaas die fluitjie, en ons hardloop.\
Ek swaai my arms en lig my knieë hoog.\
Langs my hardloop Lwazi, die vinnigste seun in ons klas.\
Die mense op die pawiljoen skree en klap hande.\
Ek hardloop so vinnig as wat ek kan.\
Aan die einde breek ek deur die lint!\
Ek het gewen, en ek kry 'n goue medalje.\
Lwazi skud my hand en sê: "Mooi so!"\
Ons drink saam koue water in die skaduwee.\
Sportdag is die lekkerste dag van die jaar!

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Wat is die skool se kleure?** Rooi en wit. / **Blou en wit. ✓** / Groen en geel. / Swart en goud. / Blou en geel.
2. **Hoe lank is die wedloop?** Vyftig meter. / Tweehonderd meter. / **Honderd meter. ✓** / Een kilometer. / Tien meter.
3. **Wie is die vinnigste seun in die klas?** Sipho. / Pieter. / Johan. / **Lwazi. ✓** / Tumi.
4. **Wat kry ek toe ek wen?** **'n Goue medalje. ✓** / 'n Beker. / 'n Sertifikaat. / Lekkers. / 'n Boek.
5. **Wat sê Lwazi?** "Ek is kwaad!" / "Weer!" / "Totsiens!" / "Ek het gewen!" / **"Mooi so!" ✓**

### Woordherkenning (10)

sportdag, skool, kleure, wedloop, hart, knieë, pawiljoen, lint, medalje, skaduwee

### Grammatika: verkleinwoorde (5)

*Gee die verkleinwoord – Bv. hond – hondjie*

1. lint – **lintjie**
2. hand – **handjie**
3. kop – **koppie**
4. hart – **hartjie**
5. stoel – **stoeltjie**

### Woordeskat (5)

1. Ons hardloop 'n \_\_\_\_\_\_ op sportdag. wedlope / **wedloop ✓** / wolk / weddenskap
2. Oupa sit op die \_\_\_\_\_\_ en kyk rugby. papier / pampoen / **pawiljoen ✓** / paviljoen
3. Die wenner kry 'n goue \_\_\_\_\_\_. **medalje ✓** / medaljes / medisyne / melk
4. Die atleet breek deur die \_\_\_\_\_\_. lip / tint / lits / **lint ✓**
5. Dit is warm; kom ons sit in die \_\_\_\_\_\_. skape / skaats / skouer / **skaduwee ✓**

## Level 2 – Afrikaans 3: Nomsa wil ruimtevaarder word

Topic: Space and stars. About 112 words, 14 sentences.

### Woordkaarte (5)

**ruimte** – Die groot, donker plek bo die lug waar die sterre en planete is. Bv. Die maan is in die ruimte. Engels: "space". *Prent: sterre en planete.*

**ruimtevaarder** – Iemand wat met 'n vuurpyl na die ruimte reis. Bv. Die ruimtevaarder loop op die maan. Een ruimtevaarder, baie ruimtevaarders. Engels: "astronaut". *Prent: 'n ruimtevaarder in 'n ruimtepak.*

**sweef** – Om stadig in die lug te dryf sonder om te val. Bv. Die ballon sweef hoog bo die huise. Ek sweef, ek het gesweef. Engels: "float". *Prent: 'n ballon in die lug.*

**aarde** – Die planeet waarop ons woon. Bv. Die aarde draai om die son. Engels: "Earth". *Prent: die aarde uit die ruimte.*

**fluister** – Om baie sag te praat. Bv. Ons fluister in die biblioteek. Ek fluister, ek het gefluister. Engels: "whisper". *Prent: 'n kind wat in iemand se oor fluister.*

### Die leesstuk

Nomsa lees 'n boek oor die ruimte.\
Sy wil eendag 'n ruimtevaarder word.\
'n Ruimtevaarder vlieg met 'n vuurpyl na die ruimte.\
In die ruimte is daar geen lug om asem te haal nie.\
Daarom dra hulle 'n spesiale pak en 'n helm.\
Alles in die ruimtetuig sweef rond.\
Selfs die ruimtevaarders sweef bo hul beddens!\
Hulle eet kos uit sakkies en drink deur 'n strooitjie.\
Deur die venster kan hulle die aarde sien.\
Die aarde lyk soos 'n groot blou-en-wit bal.\
Nomsa teken 'n vuurpyl met haar naam daarop.\
Dan maak sy haar oë toe en droom.\
Sy sien haarself tussen die sterre sweef.\
"Eendag gaan ek ook daar wees," fluister sy.

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Wat wil Nomsa eendag word?** 'n Dokter. / 'n Onderwyser. / **'n Ruimtevaarder. ✓** / 'n Vlieënier. / 'n Kok.
2. **Waarom dra ruimtevaarders 'n spesiale pak?** Omdat dit koud is. / **Omdat daar geen lug is om asem te haal nie. ✓** / Omdat dit mooi is. / Om vinnig te hardloop. / Om te swem.
3. **Hoe eet ruimtevaarders?** Van borde af. / Uit blikke. / Uit bakke. / **Uit sakkies. ✓** / Hulle eet nie.
4. **Hoe lyk die aarde uit die ruimte?** **Soos 'n groot blou-en-wit bal. ✓** / Soos 'n rooi bal. / Soos 'n groen blaar. / Soos 'n plat bord. / Soos 'n geel ster.
5. **Wat teken Nomsa?** 'n Ster. / Die maan. / Haar huis. / 'n Kat. / **'n Vuurpyl met haar naam daarop. ✓**

### Woordherkenning (10)

ruimte, ruimtevaarder, vuurpyl, spesiale, helm, sweef, sakkies, strooitjie, aarde, fluister

### Grammatika: meervoude (5)

*Gee die meervoud – Bv. hond – honde*

1. ster – **sterre**
2. planeet – **planete**
3. vuurpyl – **vuurpyle**
4. helm – **helms**
5. sak – **sakke**

### Woordeskat (5)

1. Sy wil eendag 'n \_\_\_\_\_\_ word. ruimtevaarders / **ruimtevaarder ✓** / ruimte / ruimtetuig
2. Die veer \_\_\_\_\_\_ stadig na die grond. **sweef ✓** / sweet / swem / swaan
3. Ons woon op die planeet \_\_\_\_\_\_. Aar / **Aarde ✓** / Aardig / Aarbei
4. In die biblioteek moet ons \_\_\_\_\_\_. fluit / fluweel / **fluister ✓** / flikker
5. Die sterre en planete is in die \_\_\_\_\_\_. ruim / ruite / rym / **ruimte ✓**

## Level 2 – Afrikaans 4: Kampeer by die dam

Topic: Nature and outdoors. About 109 words, 13 sentences.

### Woordkaarte (5)

**kampeer** – Om buite in 'n tent te bly en te slaap. Bv. Ons gaan in die vakansie in die berge kampeer. Ek kampeer, ek het gekampeer. Engels: "camp". *Prent: 'n tent by 'n dam.*

**kole** – Stukkies hout of steenkool wat ná 'n vuur nog gloeiend warm is. Bv. Pa braai die vleis oor die kole. Engels: "coals". *Prent: gloeiende kole.*

**skitter** – Om helder te blink met klein flitsies lig. Bv. Die diamant skitter in die son. Dit skitter, dit het geskitter. Engels: "sparkle / twinkle". *Prent: sterre in die nag.*

**slaapsak** – 'n Warm, sagte sak waarin jy slaap wanneer jy kampeer. Bv. My slaapsak is blou en warm. Een slaapsak, baie slaapsakke. Engels: "sleeping bag". *Prent: 'n kind in 'n slaapsak.*

**reier** – 'n Lang voël met lang bene wat in die water na vis soek. Bv. Die reier vang 'n vissie. Een reier, baie reiers. Engels: "heron". *Prent: 'n reier in vlak water.*

### Die leesstuk

Ons gesin gaan vir die naweek kampeer.\
Pa slaan die tent langs die dam op.\
Ek help om die penne in die grond te slaan.\
My broer maak 'n vuurtjie met droë takke.\
Ma braai wors en mielies op die kole.\
Na ete sit ons om die vuur en sing liedjies.\
Die sterre skitter helder bo ons koppe.\
Ek hoor 'n uil wat hoe-hoe roep in 'n boom.\
In die nag slaap ons in warm slaapsakke.\
Die volgende oggend word ek vroeg wakker.\
'n Mis lê soos 'n wit kombers oor die water.\
Ek sien 'n reier wat stil in die vlak water staan.\
Kampeer is die lekkerste avontuur!

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Waar slaan Pa die tent op?** **Langs die dam. ✓** / In die berge. / By die see. / In die tuin. / In die bos.
2. **Waarmee maak my broer die vuur?** Met papier. / **Met droë takke. ✓** / Met steenkool. / Met gas. / Met nat blare.
3. **Wat braai Ma?** Vis. / Hoender. / **Wors en mielies. ✓** / Tjops. / Aartappels.
4. **Watter voël hoor ek in die nag?** 'n Hoender. / 'n Reier. / 'n Papegaai. / **'n Uil. ✓** / 'n Duif.
5. **Hoe lyk die mis oor die water?** Soos grys rook. / Soos 'n blou lint. / Soos sneeu. / Soos 'n spieël. / **Soos 'n wit kombers. ✓**

### Woordherkenning (10)

kampeer, tent, penne, vuurtjie, takke, mielies, sterre, uil, slaapsakke, reier

### Grammatika: teenoorgesteldes (5)

*Gee die teenoorgestelde – Bv. groot – klein*

1. droog – **nat**
2. helder – **dof** *(accept: donker)*
3. vroeg – **laat**
4. stil – **raserig** *(accept: luid, hard)*
5. vlak – **diep**

### Woordeskat (5)

1. Ons \_\_\_\_\_\_ elke vakansie by die rivier. kameel / kamer / **kampeer ✓** / kampioen
2. Die vleis braai oor die warm \_\_\_\_\_\_. kool / **kole ✓** / koele / kolle
3. Die sterre \_\_\_\_\_\_ in die donker lug. **skitter ✓** / skiet / skinder / skaters
4. Ek kruip in my warm \_\_\_\_\_\_. slaai / slaaptyd / slaapliedjie / **slaapsak ✓**
5. Die \_\_\_\_\_\_ staan op sy lang bene in die water. reën / **reier ✓** / rei / ryer

## Level 2 – Afrikaans 5: Ek voel siek

Topic: My body and health. About 105 words, 14 sentences.

### Woordkaarte (5)

**keel** – Die deel binne-in jou nek waardeur kos en lug gaan. Bv. My keel is seer as ek te veel skree. Engels: "throat". *Prent: 'n kind wat aan sy keel vat.*

**voorkop** – Die deel van jou gesig bo jou oë. Bv. Hy vee die sweet van sy voorkop af. Een voorkop, baie voorkoppe. Engels: "forehead". *Prent: 'n hand op 'n kind se voorkop.*

**koors** – Wanneer jou liggaam te warm word omdat jy siek is. Bv. Die baba het koors en moet dokter toe. Engels: "fever". *Prent: 'n termometer.*

**liggaam** – Al die dele van 'n mens of dier: kop, arms, bene en alles. Bv. Oefening maak jou liggaam sterk. Engels: "body". *Prent: 'n kind wat strek.*

**gesond** – Wanneer jy nie siek is nie en goed voel. Bv. Vrugte en groente hou jou gesond. Teenoorgestelde: siek. Engels: "healthy / well". *Prent: 'n kind wat vrolik hardloop.*

### Die leesstuk

Vanoggend voel ek glad nie lekker nie.\
My keel is seer en my neus loop.\
Ma voel aan my voorkop.\
"Jy het koors," sê sy.\
Ek bly vandag by die huis en gaan nie skool toe nie.\
Ma maak vir my warm suurlemoen-en-heuningtee.\
Ek lê onder my sagte kombers op die bank.\
Ek slaap baie, want slaap help my liggaam om gesond te word.\
My hond Bella lê naby my voete.\
Sy weet ek is siek.\
Ek drink baie water en eet 'n bietjie sop.\
Teen die aand voel ek al 'n bietjie beter.\
Twee dae later is ek weer gesond.\
Ek stap vrolik skool toe!

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Wat is seer?** My voet. / **My keel. ✓** / My rug. / My oor. / My tand.
2. **Wat sê Ma?** "Gaan skool toe." / "Jy is lui." / **"Jy het koors." ✓** / "Gaan speel buite." / "Jy is gesond."
3. **Watter tee maak Ma?** Rooibostee. / Kamilletee. / Groen tee. / **Suurlemoen-en-heuningtee. ✓** / Mint-tee.
4. **Wie lê naby my voete?** **My hond Bella. ✓** / My kat. / My broer. / Ma. / My pop.
5. **Wanneer is ek weer gesond?** Die volgende oggend. / Na 'n week. / 'n Maand later. / Dieselfde aand. / **Twee dae later. ✓**

### Woordherkenning (10)

keel, neus, voorkop, koors, heuning, kombers, liggaam, water, sop, gesond

### Grammatika: meervoude (5)

*Gee die meervoud – Bv. hond – honde*

1. neus – **neuse**
2. bank – **banke**
3. kombers – **komberse**
4. voet – **voete**
5. dag – **dae**

### Woordeskat (5)

1. My \_\_\_\_\_\_ is seer as ek sluk. **keel ✓** / kiel / kele / kaal
2. Ma sit haar hand op my \_\_\_\_\_\_. voordeur / **voorkop ✓** / voorstoep / voorskoot
3. Die kind is siek en het hoë \_\_\_\_\_\_. koers / **koors ✓** / kors / koor
4. Oefening maak jou \_\_\_\_\_\_ sterk. liggame / lig / ligging / **liggaam ✓**
5. Vrugte en groente hou jou \_\_\_\_\_\_. gesondheid / gesig / **gesond ✓** / gesin

## Level 2 – Afrikaans 6: Die boom wat kon praat

Topic: Adventure and make-believe. About 108 words, 13 sentences.

### Woordkaarte (5)

**skielik** – Baie vinnig en onverwags. Bv. Skielik begin dit reën. Engels: "suddenly". *Prent: 'n verbaasde kind.*

**bas** – Die harde buitelaag van 'n boom se stam. Bv. Die bas van die ou boom is grof. Engels: "bark". *Prent: 'n boomstam van naby.*

**vriendelik** – Wanneer iemand gaaf en lekker is om mee te praat. Bv. Die nuwe juffrou is baie vriendelik. Teenoorgestelde: onvriendelik. Engels: "friendly". *Prent: 'n glimlaggende gesig.*

**wilg** – 'n Boom met lang, dun takke wat na die grond toe hang. Dit groei dikwels by water. Bv. Die wilg hang oor die rivier. Een wilg, baie wilgers. Engels: "willow". *Prent: 'n wilgerboom by 'n rivier.*

**belowe** – Om te sê dat jy iets beslis gaan doen. Bv. Ek belowe om my kamer skoon te maak. Ek belowe, ek het belowe. Engels: "promise". *Prent: twee kinders wat hande skud.*

### Die leesstuk

Thabo speel alleen in die bos agter sy huis.\
Skielik hoor hy 'n diep stem: "Goeiemôre, seun!"\
Hy kyk om, maar daar is niemand nie.\
Toe sien hy die ou boom se bas beweeg.\
Die boom het oë en 'n groot, vriendelike mond!\
"Ek is Oupa Wilg," sê die boom.\
"Ek is al honderd jaar oud."\
Oupa Wilg vertel vir Thabo stories oor die bos.\
Hy vertel van die uile, die jakkalse en die bye.\
Hy vra vir Thabo om die bos skoon te hou.\
Thabo belowe om al die rommel op te tel.\
Elke dag besoek hy sy nuwe vriend.\
Maar net Thabo kan die boom hoor praat.

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Waar speel Thabo?** In die park. / **In die bos agter sy huis. ✓** / By die skool. / Op die strand. / In sy kamer.
2. **Wat sê die stem?** "Wie is jy?" / "Gaan huis toe!" / **"Goeiemôre, seun!" ✓** / "Help my!" / "Totsiens!"
3. **Hoe oud is Oupa Wilg?** Tien jaar. / Vyftig jaar. / Twintig jaar. / **Honderd jaar. ✓** / Duisend jaar.
4. **Waaroor vertel Oupa Wilg stories?** **Oor die bos. ✓** / Oor die see. / Oor die stad. / Oor die ruimte. / Oor sy familie.
5. **Wat belowe Thabo?** Om die boom af te kap. / Om 'n huis te bou. / Om nie terug te kom nie. / Om die boom water te gee. / **Om al die rommel op te tel. ✓**

### Woordherkenning (10)

bos, skielik, stem, niemand, bas, vriendelike, honderd, jakkalse, rommel, belowe

### Grammatika: verkleinwoorde (5)

*Gee die verkleinwoord – Bv. hond – hondjie*

1. boom – **boompie**
2. bos – **bossie**
3. mond – **mondjie**
4. oog – **ogie**
5. uil – **uiltjie**

### Woordeskat (5)

1. \_\_\_\_\_\_ begin dit hard reën. **Skielik ✓** / Skilpad / Skiet / Skoolbus
2. Die \_\_\_\_\_\_ van die boom is grof en bruin. bus / **bas ✓** / baas / bars
3. Die juffrou is baie \_\_\_\_\_\_ met die nuwe kinders. vriendelike / **vriendelik ✓** / vriend / vriendskap
4. Ek \_\_\_\_\_\_ om my speelgoed op te pak. belofte / beloon / **belowe ✓** / behoort
5. Die \_\_\_\_\_\_ hang met sy lang takke oor die water. wolk / wild / wilgers / **wilg ✓**

## Level 2 – Afrikaans 7: Die windpomp

Topic: How things work. About 118 words, 13 sentences.

### Woordkaarte (5)

**windpomp** – 'n Hoë toring met 'n wiel wat deur die wind gedraai word om water uit die grond te pomp. Bv. Die windpomp staan langs die kraal. Een windpomp, baie windpompe. Engels: "windpump". *Prent: 'n windpomp op 'n plaas.*

**stang** – 'n Lang, dun staaf van yster of hout. Bv. Die gordyn hang aan 'n stang. Een stang, baie stange. Engels: "rod". *Prent: 'n gordynstang.*

**pomp** – Om water of lug met 'n masjien in of uit iets te druk. Bv. Pa pomp my fietsband op. Ek pomp, ek het gepomp. Engels: "pump". *Prent: 'n fietspomp.*

**krip** – 'n Lang bak waaruit diere eet of drink. Bv. Die perde drink uit die krip. Een krip, baie krippe. Engels: "trough". *Prent: koeie by 'n krip.*

**dam** – 'n Groot bak of meer waarin water gehou word. Bv. Die eende swem in die dam. Een dam, baie damme. Engels: "dam / reservoir". *Prent: 'n sementdam op 'n plaas.*

### Die leesstuk

Op Oom Koos se plaas staan 'n hoë windpomp.\
Die windpomp het 'n groot wiel met baie blaaie.\
As die wind waai, draai die wiel rond en rond.\
Die wiel is aan 'n lang stang vas.\
Die stang beweeg op en af in 'n pyp.\
So pomp dit water diep uit die grond.\
Die water loop in 'n groot sementdam.\
Die koeie en skape drink uit 'n krip naby die dam.\
'n Stert agter aan die wiel draai dit na die wind toe.\
As daar geen wind is nie, staan die wiel stil.\
Dan moet Oom Koos water uit die dam gebruik.\
Ek hou daarvan om na die draaiende wiel te kyk.\
Dit sing saggies in die wind.

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Wie se plaas is dit?** Oupa se plaas. / **Oom Koos se plaas. ✓** / Tant Sannie se plaas. / Ons plaas. / Die buurman se plaas.
2. **Wat laat die wiel draai?** Die son. / 'n Motor. / Die water. / **Die wind. ✓** / Oom Koos.
3. **Waar beweeg die stang op en af?** **In 'n pyp. ✓** / In die dam. / In die lug. / In die krip. / In die kraal.
4. **Waaruit drink die koeie en skape?** Uit 'n emmer. / Uit die rivier. / **Uit 'n krip. ✓** / Uit 'n kraan. / Uit 'n bad.
5. **Wat doen die stert?** Dit stop die wiel. / Dit pomp water. / Dit maak 'n geluid. / Dit hou die voëls weg. / **Dit draai die wiel na die wind toe. ✓**

### Woordherkenning (10)

plaas, windpomp, wiel, blaaie, stang, pyp, pomp, sementdam, krip, draaiende

### Grammatika: meervoude (5)

*Gee die meervoud – Bv. hond – honde*

1. wiel – **wiele**
2. pyp – **pype**
3. stang – **stange**
4. krip – **krippe**
5. windpomp – **windpompe**

### Woordeskat (5)

1. Op die plaas staan 'n hoë \_\_\_\_\_\_. **windpomp ✓** / windpompe / windmeter / windjie
2. Die gordyn hang aan 'n \_\_\_\_\_\_. slang / **stang ✓** / stank / stange
3. Pa \_\_\_\_\_\_ my fietsband op. pond / pop / **pomp ✓** / pompe
4. Die perde drink uit die \_\_\_\_\_\_. krap / kring / kruip / **krip ✓**
5. Die eende swem in die \_\_\_\_\_\_. dak / damp / dans / **dam ✓**

## Level 2 – Afrikaans 8: Ouma se melktert

Topic: Food and cooking. About 111 words, 13 sentences.

### Woordkaarte (5)

**kors** – Die harde buitelaag van 'n tert of brood. Bv. Die kors van die pastei is bros. Engels: "crust". *Prent: 'n tert met 'n goue kors.*

**deeg** – 'n Sagte mengsel van meel met water of botter waarmee jy brood of koek maak. Bv. Ma knie die deeg vir die brood. Engels: "dough / pastry". *Prent: hande wat deeg knie.*

**verhit** – Om iets warm te maak. Bv. Verhit die sop op die stoof. Ek verhit, ek het verhit. Engels: "heat". *Prent: 'n pot op die stoof.*

**klont** – 'n Klein, harde stukkie in iets wat glad moet wees. Bv. Die pap het klonte in. Een klont, baie klonte. Engels: "lump". *Prent: 'n lepel in pap met klonte.*

**vulsel** – Wat binne-in 'n tert of pastei kom. Bv. Die pastei se vulsel is hoender. Engels: "filling". *Prent: 'n tert wat oopgesny is.*

### Die leesstuk

Vandag leer Ouma my om melktert te bak.\
Eers maak ons die kors van meel, botter en suiker.\
Ek druk die deeg in 'n ronde bakvorm.\
Ouma sit die bakvorm vir 'n rukkie in die oond.\
Intussen verhit sy melk in 'n pot op die stoof.\
Ek klop eiers, suiker en meel saam in 'n bak.\
Ouma gooi die warm melk stadig by die eiers.\
Sy roer aanhoudend sodat dit nie klonte maak nie.\
Die vulsel word dik en glad.\
Ons gooi die vulsel in die kors.\
Dan strooi ek kaneel bo-oor.\
Die melktert moet eers afkoel voordat ons dit kan eet.\
Dit is die lekkerste tert in die hele wêreld!

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Wat leer Ouma my om te bak?** Koek. / **Melktert. ✓** / Brood. / Beskuit. / Koekies.
2. **Waarvan maak ons die kors?** Van melk en eiers. / **Van meel, botter en suiker. ✓** / Van brood en kaas. / Van suiker en kaneel. / Van sjokolade.
3. **Waar verhit Ouma die melk?** In die oond. / In die mikrogolf. / **In 'n pot op die stoof. ✓** / In die ketel. / In 'n bak.
4. **Waarom roer Ouma aanhoudend?** Sodat dit koud word. / Sodat dit lekker ruik. / Sodat dit vinnig kook. / **Sodat dit nie klonte maak nie. ✓** / Sodat dit pienk word.
5. **Wat strooi ek bo-oor?** Suiker. / Sjokolade. / Neute. / Sout. / **Kaneel. ✓**

### Woordherkenning (10)

melktert, kors, botter, deeg, bakvorm, oond, eiers, klonte, vulsel, kaneel

### Grammatika: verkleinwoorde (5)

*Gee die verkleinwoord – Bv. hond – hondjie*

1. tert – **tertjie**
2. pot – **potjie**
3. bak – **bakkie**
4. eier – **eiertjie**
5. lepel – **lepeltjie**

### Woordeskat (5)

1. Die \_\_\_\_\_\_ van die tert is bros en goudbruin. koors / **kors ✓** / kos / korf
2. Ma knie die \_\_\_\_\_\_ vir die brood. dag / **deeg ✓** / deel / dek
3. \_\_\_\_\_\_ die melk in 'n pot. Verhaal / Verhuis / Verhoog / **Verhit ✓**
4. Roer die pap, anders kry dit \_\_\_\_\_\_. **klonte ✓** / klont / klomp / klokke
5. Die tert se \_\_\_\_\_\_ is geel en romerig. vulsels / vulpen / vullis / **vulsel ✓**

## Level 2 – Afrikaans 9: Die bobbejane by die pad

Topic: Animals. About 112 words, 13 sentences.

### Woordkaarte (5)

**bobbejaan** – 'n Groot aap met 'n lang snoet wat in troppe in die berge woon. Bv. Die bobbejaan sit op die rots. Een bobbejaan, baie bobbejane. Engels: "baboon". *Prent: 'n bobbejaan op 'n rots.*

**kleintjie** – 'n Baba-dier. Bv. Die kat het drie kleintjies. Een kleintjie, baie kleintjies. Engels: "baby animal". *Prent: 'n ma-bobbejaan met haar kleintjie.*

**mannetjie** – 'n Manlike dier. Bv. Die leeumannetjie het 'n groot maanhaar. Een mannetjie, baie mannetjies. Teenoorgestelde: wyfie. Engels: "male". *Prent: 'n leeumannetjie.*

**steel** – Om iets te vat wat nie aan jou behoort nie. Bv. Die kraai steel die brood. Ek steel, ek het gesteel. Engels: "steal". *Prent: 'n voël met 'n stukkie brood.*

**enjinkap** – Die deksel voor op 'n kar wat die enjin bedek. Bv. Pa lig die enjinkap om na die olie te kyk. Engels: "bonnet". *Prent: 'n kar met die enjinkap oop.*

### Die leesstuk

Op pad see toe ry ons deur die berge.\
Skielik sien ons 'n trop bobbejane langs die pad.\
Hulle sit op die klippe en in die bome.\
'n Ma-bobbejaan dra haar kleintjie op haar rug.\
Die kleintjie hou styf vas aan haar hare.\
'n Groot mannetjie loop reguit na ons kar toe.\
"Maak die vensters toe!" roep Pa.\
Bobbejane is baie slim en kan kos uit karre steel.\
Die mannetjie klim op die enjinkap en loer by die venster in.\
Hy wys sy lang, skerp tande.\
Pa ry stadig verder, en die bobbejaan spring af.\
Ma sê ons mag nooit wilde diere kos gee nie.\
Dan leer hulle om mense te pla.

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Waarheen ry ons?** **See toe. ✓** / Berge toe. / Plaas toe. / Skool toe. / Dieretuin toe.
2. **Hoe dra die ma-bobbejaan haar kleintjie?** In haar arms. / **Op haar rug. ✓** / In haar bek. / Op haar kop. / Aan haar stert.
3. **Wat roep Pa?** "Kyk daar!" / "Gee vir hulle kos!" / **"Maak die vensters toe!" ✓** / "Klim uit!" / "Hou stil!"
4. **Waarop klim die mannetjie?** Op die dak. / Op 'n boom. / Op 'n klip. / **Op die enjinkap. ✓** / Op die kattebak.
5. **Waarom mag ons nooit wilde diere kos gee nie?** Hulle word te vet. / Hulle hou nie van kos nie. / Dit is te duur. / Hulle raak hartseer. / **Dan leer hulle om mense te pla. ✓**

### Woordherkenning (10)

berge, bobbejane, klippe, kleintjie, mannetjie, vensters, slim, enjinkap, skerp, wilde

### Grammatika: teenoorgesteldes (5)

*Gee die teenoorgestelde – Bv. groot – klein*

1. mannetjie – **wyfie**
2. slim – **dom**
3. skerp – **stomp**
4. wild – **mak**
5. toe – **oop**

### Woordeskat (5)

1. 'n \_\_\_\_\_\_ steel die piesang uit die kar. bobbejane / **bobbejaan ✓** / bobbel / boontjie
2. Die kat lek haar klein \_\_\_\_\_\_. kleinte / **kleintjie ✓** / klein / kleinkind
3. Die kraai probeer die brood \_\_\_\_\_\_. **steel ✓** / stel / steeg / stelle
4. Pa lig die \_\_\_\_\_\_ om na die olie te kyk. enjin / enkel / engel / **enjinkap ✓**
5. 'n Leeu is 'n \_\_\_\_\_\_ dier. wild / wilg / **wilde ✓** / wolde

## Level 2 – Afrikaans 10: Krieket in die agterplaas

Topic: Sport and games. About 108 words, 14 sentences.

### Woordkaarte (5)

**paaltjies** – Drie stokkies in die grond waarna die bouler in krieket mik. Bv. Die bal het die paaltjies omgestamp. Engels: "wickets". *Prent: krieketpaaltjies.*

**kolf** – Die plat stuk hout waarmee jy die bal in krieket slaan. Dit beteken ook om die bal met die kolf te slaan. Bv. Sy kolf baie goed. Een kolf, baie kolwe. Engels: "bat". *Prent: 'n krieketkolf.*

**boul** – Om die bal na die kolwer te gooi in krieket. Bv. Hy boul 'n vinnige bal. Ek boul, ek het geboul. Engels: "bowl". *Prent: 'n seun wat 'n bal boul.*

**lopie** – 'n Punt wat jy in krieket kry. Bv. Sy het vier lopies gekry. Een lopie, baie lopies. Engels: "run". *Prent: 'n telbord.*

**beurt** – Wanneer dit jou tyd is om iets te doen. Bv. Dit is jou beurt om te gooi. Engels: "turn". *Prent: kinders wat in 'n ry staan.*

### Die leesstuk

Na skool speel ek en my broers krieket in die agterplaas.\
Ons gebruik 'n ou emmer as paaltjies.\
My broer Ruan kolf eerste.\
Ek boul die bal so vinnig as wat ek kan.\
Hy slaan die bal hoog oor die heining!\
"Ses lopies!" skree hy en dans rond.\
Toe gooi ek 'n stadige bal.\
Ruan swaai sy kolf, maar hy mis.\
Die bal tref die emmer met 'n harde kletter.\
"Uit!" roep my klein boetie.\
Nou is dit my beurt om te kolf.\
Ek hou die kolf styf vas en kyk mooi na die bal.\
Ek slaan dit tussen die blombeddings deur.\
Ons speel totdat Ma ons vir aandete roep.

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Waar speel ons krieket?** In die park. / **In die agterplaas. ✓** / By die skool. / Op die strand. / In die straat.
2. **Wat gebruik ons as paaltjies?** Drie stokke. / 'n Stoel. / **'n Ou emmer. ✓** / 'n Boks. / 'n Boom.
3. **Wie kolf eerste?** Ek. / My klein boetie. / Pa. / **Ruan. ✓** / Ma.
4. **Wat roep my klein boetie?** **"Uit!" ✓** / "Ses!" / "Vang!" / "Hardloop!" / "Stop!"
5. **Tot wanneer speel ons?** Totdat dit donker word. / Totdat dit reën. / Totdat ons moeg is. / Totdat Pa kom. / **Totdat Ma ons vir aandete roep. ✓**

### Woordherkenning (10)

krieket, agterplaas, emmer, paaltjies, kolf, boul, heining, lopies, kletter, beurt

### Grammatika: meervoude (5)

*Gee die meervoud – Bv. hond – honde*

1. kolf – **kolwe**
2. bal – **balle**
3. emmer – **emmers**
4. broer – **broers**
5. lopie – **lopies**

### Woordeskat (5)

1. Die bouler mik na die \_\_\_\_\_\_. paadjies / **paaltjies ✓** / paaie / pakkies
2. Hy slaan die bal met sy \_\_\_\_\_\_. kolwe / golf / **kolf ✓** / kalf
3. Sy \_\_\_\_\_\_ 'n baie vinnige bal. **boul ✓** / bou / bol / boule
4. Hy kry ses \_\_\_\_\_\_ vir die hou. lopie / lepels / loopies / **lopies ✓**
5. Dit is jou \_\_\_\_\_\_ om te gooi. beurs / buurt / beurte / **beurt ✓**

## Level 2 – English: Ben Bakes a Cake

About 113 words, 13 sentences averaging 9 words, one sentence per line, a simple story. Font: Andika.

### Word cards (5)

**flour** – A soft white powder made from wheat. We use it to bake bread and cakes. e.g. Gran buys a bag of flour. Afrikaans: "meel". *Picture: a bag of flour.*

**oven** – A hot box in the kitchen where you bake or cook food. e.g. The bread is baking in the oven. One oven, many ovens. Afrikaans: "oond". *Picture: an oven with a cake inside.*

**icing** – A sweet, soft topping for cakes. e.g. The cake has white icing and cherries. Afrikaans: "versiersuiker". *Picture: a cupcake with icing.*

**mess** – When things are dirty or untidy. e.g. My bedroom is a mess. Afrikaans: "gemors". *Picture: a messy kitchen table.*

**surprise** – Something nice that you did not know was going to happen. e.g. The party was a big surprise for Gran. One surprise, many surprises. Afrikaans: "verrassing". *Picture: a gift box.*

### The passage

It is Mom's birthday today.\
Ben and Dad want to bake her a cake.\
They put flour, sugar and eggs in a big bowl.\
Ben mixes it with a wooden spoon.\
Dad pours it into a round tin.\
The cake bakes in the oven for half an hour.\
Soon the whole kitchen smells sweet and yummy.\
When the cake is cool, Ben spreads pink icing on top.\
He adds six candles and some chocolate sprinkles.\
Oh no, there is flour on the floor and on the dog!\
Ben and Dad quickly clean up the mess.\
Mom walks in and her eyes open wide.\
"What a beautiful surprise!" she says with a big smile.

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **Whose birthday is it?** It is Dad's birthday. / It is Ben's birthday. / **It is Mom's birthday. ✓** / It is Gran's birthday. / It is the dog's birthday.
2. **What does Ben mix with?** **He mixes with a wooden spoon. ✓** / He mixes with a big fork. / He mixes with his hands. / He mixes with a metal spoon. / He mixes with a whisk.
3. **How long does the cake bake?** It bakes for five minutes. / It bakes for ten minutes. / It bakes for an hour. / **It bakes for half an hour. ✓** / It bakes for two hours.
4. **What colour is the icing?** The icing is white. / **The icing is pink. ✓** / The icing is blue. / The icing is yellow. / The icing is brown.
5. **Where else is there flour?** It is on the table and on Dad. / It is on the window. / It is on Ben's head. / It is in the oven. / **It is on the floor and on the dog. ✓**

### Word recognition (10)

birthday, flour, sugar, bowl, wooden, oven, kitchen, icing, candles, surprise

### Grammar: opposites (5)

*Give the opposite – e.g. big – small*

1. cool – **warm** *(accept: hot)*
2. clean – **dirty** *(accept: messy)*
3. quickly – **slowly**
4. sweet – **sour** *(accept: bitter)*
5. open – **close** *(accept: shut)*

### Vocabulary (5)

1. We need \_\_\_\_\_\_ to make bread. flower / **flour ✓** / floor / four
2. Put the pie in the hot \_\_\_\_\_\_. even / often / **oven ✓** / ovens
3. Gran put chocolate \_\_\_\_\_\_ on the cupcakes. **icing ✓** / icy / iced / icicle
4. The party was a big \_\_\_\_\_\_ for Dad. surprised / sunrise / surprises / **surprise ✓**
5. After painting, the table was a \_\_\_\_\_\_. mass / **mess ✓** / miss / messy

## Level 2 – English 2: The Lost Puppy

Topic: Animals. About 119 words, 13 sentences.

### Word cards (5)

**shake** – To move quickly up and down or from side to side. e.g. I shake with cold in winter. I shake, I shook. Afrikaans: "bewe". *Picture: a shivering puppy.*

**gently** – In a soft and careful way. e.g. Gently pat the baby's back. Afrikaans: "saggies". *Picture: hands holding a kitten.*

**collar** – A band that goes around an animal's neck. e.g. My dog's collar is red. One collar, many collars. Afrikaans: "halsband". *Picture: a dog collar.*

**tag** – A small label with information on it. e.g. The price is on the tag. One tag, many tags. Afrikaans: "plaatjie". *Picture: a name tag on a collar.*

**proud** – Happy about something good that you or someone else did. e.g. Dad is proud of my school report. Afrikaans: "trots". *Picture: a smiling child with a certificate.*

### The passage

On the way home from school, Leo hears a sad little cry.\
He looks under a bush and finds a tiny puppy.\
The puppy is wet, dirty and shaking with fear.\
Leo gently picks it up and wraps it in his jersey.\
At home, Mom helps him give the puppy a warm bath.\
They give it some water and a small bowl of food.\
The puppy has a collar with a little silver tag.\
On the tag is a name and a phone number.\
Mom phones the number, and a girl answers.\
Soon the girl comes running to their house.\
"Biscuit!" she shouts, and the puppy wags its tail.\
She thanks Leo again and again.\
Leo feels proud that he helped.

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **Where does Leo find the puppy?** In a box. / **Under a bush. ✓** / In the road. / At school. / In a tree.
2. **What does Leo wrap the puppy in?** A towel. / A blanket. / **His jersey. ✓** / His bag. / A newspaper.
3. **What is on the puppy's collar?** **A little silver tag. ✓** / A bell. / A bow. / A light. / A key.
4. **What is the puppy's name?** Cookie. / Muffin. / Toffee. / **Biscuit. ✓** / Buddy.
5. **How does Leo feel at the end?** Sad. / Angry. / Scared. / Bored. / **Proud. ✓**

### Grammar: past tense (5)

3. wrap – **wrapped**
4. phone – **phoned**
5. carry – **carried**

## Level 2 – English 3: The Rock Pool

Topic: Nature and outdoors. About 110 words, 14 sentences.

### Word cards (5)

**tide** – The rising and falling of the sea, twice a day. e.g. At high tide the water covers the sand. Afrikaans: "gety". *Picture: waves on rocks.*

**explore** – To look around a place to find out about it. e.g. We explored the old castle. I explore, I explored. Afrikaans: "verken". *Picture: a child with a magnifying glass.*

**starfish** – A sea animal shaped like a star. e.g. The starfish has five arms. One starfish, many starfish. Afrikaans: "seester". *Picture: an orange starfish.*

**seaweed** – Plants that grow in the sea. e.g. The beach is covered in seaweed after the storm. Afrikaans: "seewier". *Picture: seaweed on rocks.*

**dart** – To move suddenly and quickly. e.g. The mouse darted into its hole. I dart, I darted. Afrikaans: "flits". *Picture: small fish swimming fast.*

### The passage

At low tide, Zara and her dad explore the rock pools.\
The water is clear and very still.\
Zara kneels down to look closely.\
She sees a bright orange starfish on a rock.\
A small crab hides quickly under some seaweed.\
Tiny fish dart in and out between the stones.\
Dad points to a sea anemone.\
It looks like a soft, pink flower.\
When Zara touches it gently, it closes up.\
"Always put things back where you found them," says Dad.\
Soon the waves start to come closer.\
"The tide is coming in," says Dad.\
They climb back up the rocks to the sand.\
Zara can't wait to come back tomorrow.

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **When do they explore the rock pools?** **At low tide. ✓** / At high tide. / At night. / In winter. / In the rain.
2. **What colour is the starfish?** Pink. / **Orange. ✓** / Red. / Purple. / Yellow.
3. **Where does the crab hide?** In a shell. / Under the sand. / **Under some seaweed. ✓** / In Dad's bucket. / Behind Zara.
4. **What does the sea anemone look like?** A green ball. / A white shell. / A small fish. / **A soft, pink flower. ✓** / A star.
5. **Why do they climb back to the sand?** It starts to rain. / It gets dark. / Zara is hungry. / Dad is tired. / **The tide is coming in. ✓**

### Word recognition (10)

tide, explore, clear, kneels, starfish, crab, seaweed, stones, anemone, tomorrow

### Grammar: opposites (5)

*Give the opposite – e.g. big – small*

1. low – **high**
2. clear – **cloudy** *(accept: murky, dirty)*
3. soft – **hard**
4. closer – **further** *(accept: farther)*
5. come – **go**

### Vocabulary (5)

4. The little fish \_\_\_\_\_\_ between the rocks. dirt / dark / date / **dart ✓**

## Level 2 – English 4: How a Kite Flies

Topic: How things work. About 116 words, 13 sentences.

### Word cards (5)

**diamond** – A shape with four straight sides, like a square standing on its corner. e.g. The road sign is shaped like a diamond. One diamond, many diamonds. Afrikaans: "ruit". *Picture: a diamond shape.*

**string** – A thin rope. e.g. Tie the parcel with string. Afrikaans: "tou". *Picture: a ball of string.*

**steady** – Not shaking or moving about. e.g. Hold the ladder steady. Afrikaans: "stil en stewig". *Picture: hands holding a ladder.*

**drift** – To move slowly, carried by wind or water. e.g. The leaf drifts down the river. It drifts, it drifted. Afrikaans: "dryf". *Picture: a leaf floating on water.*

**grin** – A big, wide smile. e.g. He had a grin from ear to ear. One grin, many grins. Afrikaans: "breë glimlag". *Picture: a grinning child.*

### The passage

It is a windy day, so Sam and Gran fly a kite.\
The kite is shaped like a diamond.\
It has a long, colourful tail.\
Gran holds the string, and Sam runs with the kite.\
When he lets go, the wind lifts it high into the sky.\
"The wind pushes against the kite and holds it up," Gran explains.\
"The string stops it from blowing away."\
"The tail helps the kite stay steady."\
Sam pulls the string gently to make the kite climb higher.\
The kite dips and dances above the trees.\
Then the wind stops, and the kite drifts down.\
Sam catches it before it lands.\
"Let's try again tomorrow," he says with a grin.

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **What shape is the kite?** A circle. / A square. / **A diamond. ✓** / A triangle. / A star.
2. **Who holds the string at first?** **Gran. ✓** / Sam. / Dad. / A friend. / Nobody.
3. **What does the tail do?** It makes the kite fast. / It makes a sound. / **It helps the kite stay steady. ✓** / It holds the string. / It keeps the kite dry.
4. **What happens when the wind stops?** The kite flies higher. / The kite breaks. / The kite flies away. / **The kite drifts down. ✓** / The kite spins.
5. **What does Sam say at the end?** "I don't like kites." / "Let's go home." / "Where is my kite?" / "It is too cold." / **"Let's try again tomorrow." ✓**

### Word recognition (10)

windy, kite, diamond, colourful, string, steady, higher, dances, drifts, tomorrow

### Grammar: -er words (5)

*Write the -er form – e.g. tall – taller*

1. high – **higher**
2. long – **longer**
3. big – **bigger**
4. windy – **windier**
5. happy – **happier**

4. The boat \_\_\_\_\_\_ slowly down the river. drafts / drips / drift / **drifts ✓**
5. He had a big \_\_\_\_\_\_ on his face. green / **grin ✓** / grain / grins

## Level 2 – English 5: A Trip to the Planetarium

Topic: Space and stars. About 115 words, 12 sentences.

### Word cards (5)

**planetarium** – A building with a round ceiling where you can watch a show about the stars. e.g. We learnt about comets at the planetarium. Afrikaans: "planetarium". *Picture: a dome-shaped building.*

**ceiling** – The top inside part of a room. e.g. A spider hangs from the ceiling. One ceiling, many ceilings. Afrikaans: "plafon". *Picture: a spider on a ceiling.*

**planet** – A very large, round object in space that moves around a star. e.g. Earth is a planet. One planet, many planets. Afrikaans: "planeet". *Picture: Saturn with its rings.*

**solar system** – The Sun and all the planets and moons that move around it. e.g. Jupiter is the biggest planet in our solar system. Afrikaans: "sonnestelsel". *Picture: the planets around the Sun.*

**closest** – Nearest; the least far away. e.g. This shop is the closest one to our house. Afrikaans: "naaste". *Picture: a house next to a shop.*

### The passage

Our class goes on a trip to the planetarium.\
We sit in soft chairs and look up at a big, round ceiling.\
Then the lights go out, and the ceiling fills with stars.\
A man tells us about the eight planets in our solar system.\
Mercury is the closest planet to the Sun.\
Jupiter is the biggest planet of all.\
Saturn has beautiful rings made of ice and rock.\
Mars is called the red planet.\
Our planet, Earth, is the only one we know that has life.\
The show makes us feel as if we are flying through space.\
On the bus, we talk about which planet we would like to visit.\
I choose Saturn, because I want to see its rings!

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **Where does the class go?** To the zoo. / **To the planetarium. ✓** / To the museum. / To the beach. / To a farm.
2. **How many planets are in our solar system?** Six. / Seven. / Nine. / **Eight. ✓** / Ten.
3. **Which planet is closest to the Sun?** **Mercury. ✓** / Mars. / Earth. / Jupiter. / Saturn.
4. **Which planet is called the red planet?** Jupiter. / Venus. / **Mars. ✓** / Saturn. / Mercury.
5. **Why do I choose Saturn?** It is the biggest. / It is the closest. / It is red. / It has life. / **I want to see its rings. ✓**

### Word recognition (10)

planetarium, ceiling, planets, system, closest, biggest, beautiful, rings, Earth, space

### Grammar: -est words (5)

## Level 2 – English 6: The Little Green Door

Topic: Adventure and make-believe. About 116 words, 13 sentences.

### Word cards (5)

**knock** – To hit a door with your hand so people know you are there. e.g. Please knock before you come in. I knock, I knocked. Afrikaans: "klop". *Picture: a hand knocking on a door.*

**creak** – A long, squeaky sound that old doors make. e.g. The old stairs creak when I walk on them. It creaks, it creaked. Afrikaans: "kraak". *Picture: an old wooden door.*

**peep** – To look quickly or secretly. e.g. I peeped through the curtains. I peep, I peeped. Afrikaans: "loer". *Picture: eyes peeping from behind a curtain.*

**polish** – To rub something until it shines. e.g. Dad polishes his shoes. I polish, I polished. Afrikaans: "blink vryf". *Picture: shiny shoes and a cloth.*

**sigh** – To breathe out loudly because you are sad or tired. e.g. She sighed when it started to rain. I sigh, I sighed. Afrikaans: "sug". *Picture: a child sighing at the window.*

### The passage

Mia finds a tiny green door at the bottom of the garden wall.\
It is only as big as a book.\
She kneels down and knocks three times.\
Slowly, the door creaks open.\
A little man with a pointy hat peeps out.\
"Hello! I am Pip, the garden keeper," he says.\
Pip tells Mia that he looks after the flowers at night.\
He paints the petals and polishes the ladybirds' spots.\
"But I have lost my paintbrush," he sighs.\
Mia searches in the grass until she finds it under a leaf.\
Pip jumps for joy and gives her a tiny golden seed.\
"Plant it, and something special will grow," he says.\
The next morning, a rainbow-coloured flower is waiting for her.

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **What colour is the door?** Red. / Blue. / **Green. ✓** / Yellow. / Brown.
2. **How many times does Mia knock?** Once. / Twice. / Four times. / **Three times. ✓** / Five times.
3. **What is the little man's name?** **Pip. ✓** / Pop. / Tip. / Pim. / Bip.
4. **What has Pip lost?** His hat. / His key. / **His paintbrush. ✓** / His seed. / His ladybird.
5. **What grows from the seed?** A tall tree. / A mushroom. / A tiny door. / A beanstalk. / **A rainbow-coloured flower. ✓**

### Word recognition (10)

garden, knocks, slowly, creaks, pointy, keeper, petals, polishes, paintbrush, golden

*Add -ly – e.g. slow – slowly*

2. sad – **sadly**
3. soft – **softly**
4. kind – **kindly**
5. brave – **bravely**

## Level 2 – English 7: The Big Match

Topic: Sport and games. About 112 words, 14 sentences.

### Word cards (5)

**match** – A game between two teams. e.g. We watched the rugby match on TV. One match, many matches. Afrikaans: "wedstryd". *Picture: two netball teams on a court.*

**coach** – A person who trains a team or a player. e.g. The coach showed us how to pass. One coach, many coaches. Afrikaans: "afrigter". *Picture: a coach with a whistle.*

**score** – To get a point or goal in a game. e.g. She scored three goals. I score, I scored. Afrikaans: "aanteken". *Picture: a ball going through a netball ring.*

**draw** – When both teams have the same score. e.g. The game ended in a draw. Afrikaans: "gelykop". *Picture: a scoreboard showing 2–2.*

**aim** – To point something at what you want to hit. e.g. Aim the ball at the hoop. I aim, I aimed. Afrikaans: "mik". *Picture: a girl aiming a ball at a ring.*

### The passage

Today is the netball match against Hill Park School.\
Amy plays goal shooter for her team.\
Her job is to throw the ball into the net.\
Before the game, the coach gives them a pep talk.\
"Pass the ball, and never give up," she says.\
The whistle blows, and the game begins.\
The other team is fast and scores two goals.\
Amy's team works hard to catch up.\
With one minute left, the score is a draw.\
Thandi passes the ball to Amy.\
Amy aims carefully and throws.\
The ball swirls around the ring and drops through the net!\
The team cheers and hugs each other.\
They have won by one goal!

3. **How many goals does the other team score first?** One. / Three. / **Two. ✓** / Four. / Five.
4. **Who passes the ball to Amy?** The coach. / Lisa. / Zola. / **Thandi. ✓** / Mom.
5. **By how much does Amy's team win?** By two goals. / By three goals. / By ten goals. / They lose. / **By one goal. ✓**

## Level 2 – English 8: Jake's Broken Arm

Topic: My body and health. About 115 words, 12 sentences.

### Word cards (5)

**X-ray** – A special photo that shows the inside of your body. e.g. The X-ray showed my bones. Afrikaans: "X-straal". *Picture: an X-ray of a hand.*

**bone** – One of the hard parts inside your body. e.g. The dog chews a bone. One bone, many bones. Afrikaans: "been". *Picture: a skeleton's arm.*

**cast** – A hard cover that keeps a broken bone still. e.g. She wore a cast on her leg. One cast, many casts. Afrikaans: "gipsverband". *Picture: an arm in a white cast.*

**heal** – To get better after being hurt or sick. e.g. The cut on my knee healed quickly. It heals, it healed. Afrikaans: "genees". *Picture: a plaster on a knee.*

**sign** – To write your name in your own way. e.g. Please sign your name here. I sign, I signed. Afrikaans: "teken". *Picture: names written on a cast.*

### The passage

Jake falls off the jungle gym at break time.\
His arm hurts so much that he starts to cry.\
The teacher calls his mom, and they go to the hospital.\
A kind nurse takes an X-ray of his arm.\
The X-ray shows a crack in one of his bones.\
"Your arm is broken," says the doctor gently.\
She puts a hard white cast on his arm.\
"The cast will keep your bone still while it heals," she explains.\
Jake has to wear the cast for six weeks.\
At school, all his friends sign their names on it.\
When the cast comes off, Jake's arm is strong again.\
He is careful on the jungle gym now!

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **Where does Jake fall?** **Off the jungle gym. ✓** / Off his bike. / Down the stairs. / Out of a tree. / Off a chair.
2. **Who takes an X-ray of his arm?** The teacher. / **A kind nurse. ✓** / His mom. / The doctor. / A friend.
3. **What colour is the cast?** Blue. / Pink. / **White. ✓** / Green. / Black.
4. **How long does Jake wear the cast?** Two weeks. / Four weeks. / Eight weeks. / **Six weeks. ✓** / Ten weeks.
5. **What do his friends do?** They paint it. / They hide it. / They wash it. / They draw on the walls. / **They sign their names on it. ✓**

### Word recognition (10)

jungle, hospital, nurse, bones, broken, doctor, cast, heals, weeks, careful

2. tooth – **teeth**
3. child – **children**
4. man – **men**
