## Level 2 – English 9: Market Day

Topic: Food and cooking. About 113 words, 13 sentences.

### Word cards (5)

**market** – A place where people sell food and other things, often outside. e.g. We buy fresh fish at the market. One market, many markets. Afrikaans: "mark". *Picture: a busy outdoor market.*

**stall** – A table or small stand where things are sold. e.g. The fruit stall has juicy mangoes. One stall, many stalls. Afrikaans: "stalletjie". *Picture: a fruit stall.*

**choose** – To pick the one you want. e.g. Choose a book to read. I choose, I chose. Afrikaans: "kies". *Picture: a hand picking an apple.*

**ripe** – Ready to eat. e.g. The bananas are yellow and ripe. Opposite: unripe. Afrikaans: "ryp". *Picture: green and ripe bananas.*

**change** – The money you get back when you pay too much. e.g. Here is your change. Afrikaans: "kleingeld". *Picture: coins in a hand.*

### The passage

Every Saturday, Lebo goes to the market with her gran.\
The market is busy and full of colours.\
There are piles of red tomatoes and yellow mealies.\
A man sells fresh bread from a big basket.\
Gran likes to choose her own fruit and vegetables.\
She squeezes the avocados to see if they are ripe.\
Lebo carries the bag and counts the money.\
"That will be forty rand," says the lady at the stall.\
Gran pays and gets two coins as change.\
Next, they buy a small pot of honey.\
At home, Lebo and Gran make a big salad.\
They eat it with warm bread and honey.\
Market day is Lebo's favourite day.

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **When does Lebo go to the market?** **Every Saturday. ✓** / Every Sunday. / Every Monday. / Every day. / Once a month.
2. **What colour are the mealies?** White. / **Yellow. ✓** / Green. / Red. / Brown.
3. **Why does Gran squeeze the avocados?** To make juice. / To clean them. / **To see if they are ripe. ✓** / To break them. / Because she is cross.
4. **How much do they pay at the stall?** Twenty rand. / Thirty rand. / Fifty rand. / **Forty rand. ✓** / Sixty rand.
5. **What do they eat with the salad?** Rice. / Chips. / Meat. / Soup. / **Warm bread and honey. ✓**

### Word recognition (10)

market, busy, tomatoes, mealies, basket, choose, avocados, carries, change, honey

### Grammar: plurals (5)

*Give the plural – e.g. cat – cats*

1. tomato – **tomatoes**
2. potato – **potatoes**
3. basket – **baskets**
4. lady – **ladies**
5. loaf – **loaves**

### Vocabulary (5)

1. We buy fresh eggs at the \_\_\_\_\_\_. marker / **market ✓** / marble / mark
2. The man sells hats at his \_\_\_\_\_\_. stale / still / **stall ✓** / tall
3. You may \_\_\_\_\_\_ one sweet from the jar. **choose ✓** / chose / cheese / chooses
4. The mango is soft and \_\_\_\_\_\_. rip / rope / ripen / **ripe ✓**
5. The shopkeeper gave me my \_\_\_\_\_\_. chance / charge / chains / **change ✓**

## Level 2 – English 10: Meerkat Watch

Topic: Animals. About 111 words, 12 sentences.

### Word cards (5)

**burrow** – A hole or tunnel in the ground where an animal lives. e.g. The rabbit hides in its burrow. One burrow, many burrows. Afrikaans: "gat". *Picture: a meerkat at its burrow.*

**guard** – Someone who watches over a place to keep it safe. e.g. A guard stands at the gate. One guard, many guards. Afrikaans: "wag". *Picture: a meerkat standing on a rock.*

**beetle** – An insect with a hard, shiny back. e.g. A black beetle crawls on the path. One beetle, many beetles. Afrikaans: "kewer". *Picture: a beetle.*

**warn** – To tell someone about danger. e.g. The sign warns us about the deep water. I warn, I warned. Afrikaans: "waarsku". *Picture: a warning sign.*

**dive** – To jump or go down quickly, head first. e.g. The boy dives into the pool. I dive, I dived. Afrikaans: "duik". *Picture: a child diving into a pool.*

### The passage

In the Kalahari, a family of meerkats lives in a burrow.\
Every morning, they come out to sit in the warm sun.\
They stand up on their back legs to look around.\
One meerkat always keeps guard while the others look for food.\
They dig in the sand for beetles, worms and scorpions.\
Suddenly, the guard sees an eagle in the sky.\
He gives a loud, sharp bark to warn the others.\
In a flash, all the meerkats dive into the burrow.\
The eagle flies away hungry.\
After a while, a small head peeps out.\
Soon the whole family is back in the sun.\
Meerkats keep each other safe by working together.

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **Where do the meerkats live?** In a tree. / **In a burrow. ✓** / In a nest. / In a cave. / By a river.
2. **What does one meerkat do while the others look for food?** It sleeps. / It eats. / **It keeps guard. ✓** / It digs. / It plays.
3. **What does the guard see in the sky?** A plane. / A cloud. / A crow. / **An eagle. ✓** / A kite.
4. **How does the guard warn the others?** **With a loud, sharp bark. ✓** / By jumping. / By running away. / By singing. / By waving.
5. **How do meerkats keep each other safe?** By fighting. / By hiding alone. / By running far away. / By sleeping all day. / **By working together. ✓**

### Word recognition (10)

meerkats, family, burrow, morning, guard, beetles, scorpions, eagle, warn, together

### Grammar: adding -ing (5)

*Add -ing – e.g. run – running*

1. dig – **digging**
2. sit – **sitting**
3. dive – **diving**
4. look – **looking**
5. stand – **standing**

### Vocabulary (5)

1. The rabbit hides in its \_\_\_\_\_\_. borrow / barrow / **burrow ✓** / furrow
2. A shiny black \_\_\_\_\_\_ crawls on the leaf. bottle / **beetle ✓** / beetles / battle
3. The sign \_\_\_\_\_\_ us about the deep water. warms / worns / warn / **warns ✓**
4. The duck \_\_\_\_\_\_ under the water to catch food. **dives ✓** / drives / dimes / dive
5. A \_\_\_\_\_\_ stands at the gate all night. card / gourd / guards / **guard ✓**

## Level 3 – Afrikaans: Karel se groentetuin

About 162 words, 13 sentences, one sentence per line. This is the lesson in the prototype.

### Woordkaarte (6)

**spit** – Om die grond met 'n graaf om te draai en los te maak. Bv. Pa spit die grond voor hy die blomme plant. Ek spit, ek het gespit. Engels: "to dig".

**kompos** – Verrotte blare, gras en groenteskille wat die grond ryk maak. Dit is goed vir plante. Bv. Ma gooi die ou groenteskille op die komposhoop. Engels: "compost".

**onkruid** – Plante wat groei waar jy hulle nie wil hê nie. Dit steel water en kos van die ander plante. Bv. Ons trek elke Saterdag die onkruid in die tuin uit. Engels: "weeds".

**versigtig** – Wanneer jy iets stadig en mooi doen sodat niks breek of seerkry nie. Bv. Dra die eiers versigtig, anders breek hulle. Teenoorgestelde: onversigtig. Engels: "careful / carefully".

**heining** – 'n Draad- of houtmuur om 'n tuin of plaas. Dit hou diere in of uit. Bv. Die koei staan by die heining en kyk na ons. Een heining, baie heinings. Engels: "fence".

**gieter** – 'n Houer met 'n lang tuit waarmee jy plante water gee. Bv. Die gieter is swaar as dit vol water is. Een gieter, baie gieters. Engels: "watering can".

*Extra cards for more support:* sonnig, opgewonde

### Die leesstuk

Karel woon saam met sy ouma op 'n klein plasie buite die dorp.\
Een Saterdagoggend vra hy vir Ouma of hy sy eie groentetuin mag maak.\
Ouma lag en gee vir hom 'n graaf, 'n hark en 'n pakkie saad.\
Eers moet Karel 'n sonnige plek kies, want groente het baie lig nodig.\
Dan spit hy die grond om en haal al die klippe en onkruid uit.\
Ouma wys hom hoe om kompos deur die grond te meng.\
"Kompos is soos kos vir die plante," verduidelik sy.\
Karel maak netjiese rytjies en druk die saadjies versigtig in die grond.\
Hy plant wortels, boontjies en 'n paar pampoene langs die heining.\
Elke middag na skool gee hy die tuin water met 'n groen gieter.\
Na twee weke sien hy klein groen blaartjies wat uit die grond steek.\
Karel is so opgewonde dat hy sommer die hele familie bel.\
"Wag maar," sê Ouma, "die lekkerste deel is wanneer ons die eerste groente kan eet!"

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Saam met wie woon Karel?** Karel woon saam met sy ma. / sy oupa. / **sy ouma. ✓** / sy tannie. / sy broer.
2. **Waarom moet Karel 'n sonnige plek kies?** Want groente het baie **lig ✓** / skaduwee / wind / reën / sand nodig.
3. **Wat sê Ouma is kompos vir die plante?** Kompos is soos water / medisyne / 'n kombers / **kos ✓** / speelgoed vir die plante.
4. **Wat plant Karel langs die heining?** Hy plant 'n paar mielies / **pampoene ✓** / blomme / bome / aartappels langs die heining.
5. **Hoe lank duur dit voor Karel die eerste blaartjies sien?** Dit duur twee dae / een week / **twee weke ✓** / drie weke / twee maande.

### Woordherkenning (10)

onkruid, kompos, gieter, heining, versigtig, groentetuin, sonnige, saadjies, blaartjies, opgewonde

### Grammatika: verkleinwoorde (5)

*Gee die verkleinwoord – Bv. hond – hondjie*

1. blaar – **blaartjie**
2. saad – **saadjie**
3. tuin – **tuintjie**
4. ry – **rytjie**
5. klip – **klippie**

### Woordeskat (5)

1. Oupa gebruik 'n \_\_\_\_\_\_ om die blomme water te gee. **gieter ✓** / gieters / gitaar / giet
2. Die hond kan nie uitkom nie, want daar is 'n hoë \_\_\_\_\_\_ om die erf. heuning / **heining ✓** / heinings / horing
3. Ons moet die \_\_\_\_\_\_ uittrek, anders kry die blomme nie genoeg water nie. kruie / onrus / **onkruid ✓** / kruiwa
4. Sy maak die deur \_\_\_\_\_\_ oop sodat die baba nie wakker word nie. onversigtig / versigtige / vinnig / **versigtig ✓**
5. Ma gooi die groenteskille op die \_\_\_\_\_\_, want dit maak die grond ryk. kompas / **komposhoop ✓** / kombuis / komposhope

## Level 3 – Afrikaans 2: Die dolfyne in die baai

Topic: Animals. About 165 words, 16 sentences.

### Woordkaarte (6)

**soogdier** – 'n Dier wat sy kleintjies met melk voed. Mense, honde en walvisse is soogdiere. Bv. Die olifant is die grootste soogdier op land. Een soogdier, baie soogdiere. Engels: "mammal".

**blaasgat** – Die gat bo-op 'n dolfyn of walvis se kop waardeur hy asem haal. Bv. Water spuit uit die walvis se blaasgat. Engels: "blowhole".

**baai** – 'n Deel van die see wat in die land inkom, met land aan drie kante. Bv. Die bote lê veilig in die baai. Een baai, baie baaie. Engels: "bay".

**kalfie** – Die kleintjie van 'n koei, olifant, walvis of dolfyn. Bv. Die koei lek haar kalfie. Een kalfie, baie kalfies. Engels: "calf".

**verduidelik** – Om iets so te sê dat iemand anders dit kan verstaan. Bv. Juffrou verduidelik die som op die bord. Ek verduidelik, ek het verduidelik. Engels: "explain".

**hawe** – 'n Plek by die see waar bote en skepe veilig kan vasmeer. Bv. Die vissersbote kom saans terug hawe toe. Een hawe, baie hawens. Engels: "harbour".

*Extra cards for more support:* kaptein, seebioloog

### Die leesstuk

Tertia en haar pa gaan met 'n boot die see in by Plettenbergbaai.\
Hulle hoop om dolfyne te sien, want daar is baie in die baai.\
Die kaptein sê dolfyne is nie visse nie, maar soogdiere soos ons.\
Hulle moet bo die water kom om asem te haal.\
Hulle haal asem deur 'n blaasgat bo-op hul kop.\
Skielik skree Tertia: "Kyk, daar is hulle!"\
'n Groep van twintig dolfyne swem vinnig langs die boot.\
Party spring hoog uit die water en duik weer netjies in.\
Die kaptein verduidelik dat so 'n groep 'n skool genoem word.\
Dolfyne praat met mekaar deur klikgeluide en fluitgeluide te maak.\
'n Moeder-dolfyn swem met haar kalfie naby die boot verby.\
Die kalfie bly altyd styf teen sy ma se sy.\
Tertia neem 'n foto en glimlag van oor tot oor.\
"Eendag wil ek 'n seebioloog word," sê sy vir haar pa.\
Op pad terug na die hawe sing sy die hele tyd.\
Dit was die beste dag van haar vakansie!

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Waar gaan Tertia en haar pa met die boot uit?** By Kaapstad. / **By Plettenbergbaai. ✓** / By Durban. / By Hermanus. / By Mosselbaai.
2. **Waarom moet dolfyne bo die water kom?** Om te eet. / Om te slaap. / **Om asem te haal. ✓** / Om warm te word. / Om te speel.
3. **Wat noem ons 'n groep dolfyne?** 'n Trop. / 'n Swerm. / 'n Span. / **'n Skool. ✓** / 'n Klas.
4. **Hoe praat dolfyne met mekaar?** **Met klikgeluide en fluitgeluide. ✓** / Hulle sing. / Hulle blaf. / Hulle spring. / Met hul vinne.
5. **(Dinkvraag) Waarom wil Tertia 'n seebioloog word?** Haar pa sê sy moet. / Sy hou van bote. / Sy wil 'n kaptein wees. / Sy is bang vir die see. / **Sy het die dolfyne so baie geniet. ✓**

### Woordherkenning (10)

boot, dolfyne, soogdiere, blaasgat, skielik, twintig, verduidelik, kalfie, glimlag, hawe

### Grammatika: teenoorgesteldes (5)

*Gee die teenoorgestelde – Bv. groot – klein*

1. hoog – **laag**
2. naby – **ver**
3. beste – **slegste**
4. altyd – **nooit**
5. styf – **los**

### Woordeskat (5)

1. 'n Walvis is 'n \_\_\_\_\_\_ en nie 'n vis nie. **soogdier ✓** / soogdiere / sooi / soet
2. Die dolfyn haal asem deur sy \_\_\_\_\_\_. blaaskans / **blaasgat ✓** / blaasorkes / blaas
3. Die skepe lê veilig in die \_\_\_\_\_\_. hawer / hawik / **hawe ✓** / have
4. Die koei lek haar klein \_\_\_\_\_\_. kalkoen / kalk / kaftan / **kalfie ✓**
5. Kan jy asseblief die som vir my \_\_\_\_\_\_? verdwyn / verdubbel / verduideliking / **verduidelik ✓**

## Level 3 – Afrikaans 3: Liam se eerste fietswedren

Topic: Sport and games. About 165 words, 16 sentences.

### Woordkaarte (6)

**bult** – 'n Lae heuwel of 'n pad wat opdraand gaan. Bv. Ons stoot ons fietse teen die bult uit. Een bult, baie bulte. Engels: "hill / slope".

**wegspringplek** – Die plek waar 'n wedren begin. Bv. Die atlete staan reg by die wegspringplek. Engels: "starting line".

**toeter** – Iets wat 'n harde geluid maak om 'n teken te gee of mense te waarsku. Bv. Die kar se toeter is baie hard. Een toeter, baie toeters. Engels: "hooter".

**opgee** – Om op te hou probeer omdat iets te moeilik is. Bv. Moenie opgee nie, jy kan dit doen! Ek gee op, ek het opgegee. Engels: "give up".

**rat** – Die deel van 'n fiets of kar wat jou help om makliker of vinniger te ry. Bv. Sit die fiets in 'n ligter rat vir die bult. Een rat, baie ratte. Engels: "gear".

**eindstreep** – Die lyn waar 'n wedren eindig. Bv. Sy hardloop eerste oor die eindstreep. Engels: "finish line".

*Extra cards for more support:* afdraand, fietsryer

### Die leesstuk

Liam het maande lank vir sy eerste fietswedren geoefen.\
Elke middag na skool het hy teen die steil bult agter sy huis uitgetrap.\
Die oggend van die wedren staan honderde fietsryers by die wegspringplek.\
Almal dra helms, handskoene en helderkleurige klere.\
Liam se hart klop vinnig terwyl hy vir die toeter wag.\
Toe die toeter blaas, trap hy so hard as wat hy kan.\
Die eerste tien kilometer is plat, en hy ry maklik saam met die groep.\
Maar dan kom die lang, steil bult.\
Sy bene brand, en hy wil amper opgee.\
Toe onthou hy wat sy afrigter gesê het: "Ry een trap op 'n slag."\
Hy skakel na 'n ligter rat en trap stadig maar seker boontoe.\
Bo-op die bult juig 'n klomp mense langs die pad.\
Die laaste deel gaan afdraand, en die wind waai deur sy helm.\
Liam ry oor die eindstreep met 'n groot glimlag.\
Hy het nie gewen nie, maar hy het nie opgegee nie.\
Dit is vir hom die belangrikste.

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Waar het Liam geoefen?** In die park. / **Teen die steil bult agter sy huis. ✓** / By die skool. / Op die strand. / In 'n gimnasium.
2. **Wat dra die fietsryers?** Sonbrille en hoede. / Swembroeke. / **Helms, handskoene en helderkleurige klere. ✓** / Skooldrag. / Reënjasse.
3. **Wat gebeur met Liam op die steil bult?** Hy val van sy fiets af. / Sy fiets breek. / Hy haal almal in. / **Sy bene brand, en hy wil amper opgee. ✓** / Hy stop om te rus.
4. **Wat het sy afrigter gesê?** **"Ry een trap op 'n slag." ✓** / "Ry vinniger!" / "Jy kan maar opgee." / "Stop en rus." / "Moenie val nie."
5. **(Dinkvraag) Wat leer ons uit die storie?** Net wen is belangrik. / Fietsry is gevaarlik. / Bulte is maklik. / Jy hoef nie te oefen nie. / **Dit is belangrik om nie op te gee nie. ✓**

### Woordherkenning (10)

fietswedren, geoefen, steil, bult, helms, handskoene, toeter, opgee, eindstreep, belangrikste

### Grammatika: verlede tyd (5)

*Skryf die sin in die verlede tyd – Bv. Ek eet. – Ek het geëet.* (The site should ignore a missing full stop.)

1. Liam oefen elke dag. – **Liam het elke dag geoefen.**
2. Die mense juig. – **Die mense het gejuig.**
3. Hy ry oor die eindstreep. – **Hy het oor die eindstreep gery.**
4. Die wind waai. – **Die wind het gewaai.**
5. Ek wag vir die toeter. – **Ek het vir die toeter gewag.**

### Woordeskat (5)

1. Ons stoot ons fietse teen die steil \_\_\_\_\_\_ uit. **bult ✓** / bul / buit / bolt
2. Die kar se \_\_\_\_\_\_ maak 'n harde geluid. toets / **toeter ✓** / toring / tetter
3. Moenie \_\_\_\_\_\_ nie; jy is amper daar! opgaan / opgewek / **opgee ✓** / opgeë
4. Sit die fiets in 'n ligter \_\_\_\_\_\_. rot / rit / ratte / **rat ✓**
5. Sy hardloop eerste oor die \_\_\_\_\_\_. eindeloos / eindtoets / eintlik / **eindstreep ✓**

## Level 3 – Afrikaans 4: 'n Nag by die sterrewag

Topic: Space and stars. About 164 words, 15 sentences.

### Woordkaarte (6)

**sterrewag** – 'n Gebou met groot teleskope waar mense die sterre en planete bestudeer. Bv. Die sterrewag staan bo-op 'n heuwel. Een sterrewag, baie sterrewagte. Engels: "observatory".

**teleskoop** – 'n Instrument wat dinge wat baie ver is groter en duideliker laat lyk. Bv. Deur die teleskoop kan ek die maan sien. Een teleskoop, baie teleskope. Engels: "telescope".

**versamel** – Om dinge bymekaar te maak. Bv. My broer versamel seëls. Ek versamel, ek het versamel. Engels: "collect".

**wetenskaplike** – Iemand wat dinge ondersoek en toetse doen om meer oor die wêreld te leer. Bv. Die wetenskaplike kyk deur 'n mikroskoop. Een wetenskaplike, baie wetenskaplikes. Engels: "scientist".

**bestudeer** – Om iets baie noukeurig te leer en te ondersoek. Bv. Sy bestudeer die kaart voor die reis. Ek bestudeer, ek het bestudeer. Engels: "study".

**krater** – 'n Groot, ronde gat in die grond, soos op die maan. Bv. Die maan is vol kraters. Een krater, baie kraters. Engels: "crater".

*Extra cards for more support:* yskoud, duidelik

### Die leesstuk

Mia se klas gaan op 'n toer na die groot sterrewag by Sutherland.\
Sutherland is 'n klein dorpie in die Karoo, ver van die stadsliggies af.\
Die lug is hier so donker en helder dat 'n mens duisende sterre kan sien.\
Die gids wys vir hulle SALT, die grootste teleskoop in Afrika.\
Die teleskoop se spieël is amper so breed soos 'n klaskamer.\
Dit versamel lig van sterre wat baie, baie ver weg is.\
Wetenskaplikes van oor die hele wêreld kom hierheen om die ruimte te bestudeer.\
Die aand kyk die kinders self deur 'n kleiner teleskoop.\
Mia sien die kraters op die maan vir die eerste keer duidelik.\
Toe sien sy Saturnus met sy pragtige ringe.\
"Dit lyk net soos 'n prentjie in my boek!" roep sy uit.\
Dit is baie koud, want die Karoo word snags yskoud.\
Die kinders drink warm sjokolade om warm te bly.\
Op pad terug na die bus kyk Mia heeltyd na bo.\
Sy sal hierdie nag nooit vergeet nie.

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Waarheen gaan Mia se klas?** Na die planetarium in Kaapstad. / **Na die sterrewag by Sutherland. ✓** / Na die dieretuin. / Na 'n museum. / Na 'n plaas.
2. **Waarom kan 'n mens so baie sterre by Sutherland sien?** Omdat daar baie liggies is. / Omdat dit in die somer is. / **Omdat die lug donker en helder is, ver van die stadsliggies af. ✓** / Omdat die maan vol is. / Omdat dit reën.
3. **Wat is SALT?** 'n Vuurpyl. / 'n Planeet. / Die gids se naam. / **Die grootste teleskoop in Afrika. ✓** / 'n Dorp.
4. **Watter planeet sien Mia?** Mars. / Jupiter. / **Saturnus. ✓** / Venus. / Mercurius.
5. **(Dinkvraag) Hoe voel Mia oor die nag?** Verveeld. / Bang. / Moeg en kwaad. / Teleurgesteld. / **Sy is beïndruk en sal dit altyd onthou. ✓**

### Woordherkenning (10)

sterrewag, dorpie, helder, duisende, teleskoop, spieël, wetenskaplikes, kraters, Saturnus, vergeet

### Grammatika: trappe van vergelyking (5)

*Voltooi die trappe van vergelyking – Bv. klein – kleiner – kleinste*

1. groot – **groter** – **grootste**
2. helder – **helderder** – **helderste**
3. koud – **kouer** – **koudste**
4. ver – **verder** – **verste**
5. donker – **donkerder** – **donkerste**

### Woordeskat (5)

1. Deur die \_\_\_\_\_\_ lyk die maan baie groot. telefoon / **teleskoop ✓** / televisie / telegram
2. Die \_\_\_\_\_\_ ondersoek hoe plante groei. **wetenskaplike ✓** / wetenskap / wetsontwerp / wetenskaplik
3. My broer \_\_\_\_\_\_ ou muntstukke. versameling / versuim / **versamel ✓** / verseël
4. Die maan is vol groot, ronde \_\_\_\_\_\_. krater / karakters / kratte / **kraters ✓**
5. Die sterrekundiges werk by die \_\_\_\_\_\_. sterretjie / sterrekunde / sterrebeeld / **sterrewag ✓**

## Level 3 – Afrikaans 5: Die geluid op die solder

Topic: Adventure and make-believe. About 165 words, 16 sentences.

### Woordkaarte (6)

**solder** – Die ruimte onder die dak van 'n huis, bo die plafon. Bv. Ouma bêre die ou klere op die solder. Een solder, baie solders. Engels: "attic".

**vreemd** – Snaaks of anders as gewoonlik; iets wat jy nie ken nie. Bv. Daar is 'n vreemde reuk in die kombuis. Engels: "strange".

**spook** – 'n Gees uit stories wat glo rondloop en mense bang maak. Bv. In die storie woon 'n spook in die kasteel. Een spook, baie spoke. Engels: "ghost".

**toestemming** – Wanneer iemand vir jou sê jy mag iets doen. Bv. Jy het jou ouers se toestemming nodig om te gaan. Engels: "permission".

**spinnerak** – Die dun web wat 'n spinnekop maak. Bv. Daar is 'n groot spinnerak in die hoek. Een spinnerak, baie spinnerakke. Engels: "cobweb".

**verlig** – Wanneer jy nie meer bekommerd of bang is nie. Bv. Ma was verlig toe die hond weer tuis kom. Engels: "relieved".

*Extra cards for more support:* stowwerig, rustig

### Die leesstuk

Elke nag hoor Anri 'n vreemde geluid op die solder.\
Dit klink soos iemand wat saggies rondloop en krap.\
Haar boetie Jan is seker dit is 'n spook.\
"Spoke bestaan nie," sê Anri, maar sy is self ook 'n bietjie bang.\
Een Saterdagmiddag besluit hulle om die raaisel op te los.\
Met 'n flits en Pa se toestemming klim hulle die leer op.\
Die solder is donker, stowwerig en vol ou bokse.\
Spinnerakke hang soos gordyne van die dakbalke af.\
Skielik hoor hulle weer die krapgeluid agter 'n ou tas.\
Jan gryp Anri se arm so styf dat dit seer is.\
Stadig skuif Anri die tas eenkant toe en skyn met die flits.\
Daar, in 'n nes van ou koerante, lê 'n kat met vier klein katjies!\
Die katjies miaau en knip hul oë teen die lig.\
"Dis ons spook!" lag Jan verlig.\
Pa bring 'n mandjie, en die katfamilie trek in die warm waskamer in.\
Van toe af slaap almal in die huis weer rustig.

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Waar hoor Anri die geluid?** Onder die bed. / **Op die solder. ✓** / In die kombuis. / In die tuin. / In die motorhuis.
2. **Wat dink Jan is die geluid?** 'n Muis. / Die wind. / **'n Spook. ✓** / Pa. / 'n Uil.
3. **Wat neem hulle saam op die leer?** 'n Kers. / 'n Besem. / 'n Kamera. / **'n Flits. ✓** / 'n Stok.
4. **Wat kry hulle agter die ou tas?** **'n Kat met vier klein katjies. ✓** / 'n Muis. / 'n Uil. / 'n Spook. / 'n Hond.
5. **(Dinkvraag) Hoe voel Jan aan die einde?** Bang. / Kwaad. / Hartseer. / Verveeld. / **Verlig. ✓**

### Woordherkenning (10)

solder, vreemde, spook, toestemming, flits, stowwerig, spinnerakke, dakbalke, katjies, verlig

## Level 3 – Afrikaans 6: Die potjiekos-kompetisie

Topic: Food and cooking. About 161 words, 15 sentences.

### Woordkaarte (6)

**kompetisie** – 'n Geleentheid waar mense teen mekaar meeding om te sien wie die beste is. Bv. Sy het die sangkompetisie gewen. Een kompetisie, baie kompetisies. Engels: "competition".

**bestanddeel** – Een van die goed wat jy nodig het om iets te kook of te bak. Bv. Meel is 'n bestanddeel van brood. Een bestanddeel, baie bestanddele. Engels: "ingredient".

**laag** – Een vlak van iets wat bo-op 'n ander lê. Bv. Die koek het drie lae. Een laag, baie lae. Engels: "layer".

**prut** – Om stadig en saggies te kook. Bv. Die sop prut op die stoof. Dit prut, dit het geprut. Engels: "simmer".

**beoordelaar** – Iemand wat besluit wie in 'n kompetisie wen. Bv. Die beoordelaars proe al die koeke. Een beoordelaar, baie beoordelaars. Engels: "judge".

**sertifikaat** – 'n Spesiale papier wat jy kry as jy iets goed gedoen het. Bv. Ek het 'n sertifikaat vir goeie lees gekry. Een sertifikaat, baie sertifikate. Engels: "certificate".

*Extra cards for more support:* gietyster, waarsku

### Die leesstuk

Elke jaar hou ons dorp 'n potjiekos-kompetisie op die rugbyveld.\
Vanjaar doen ek en Oupa vir die eerste keer saam mee.\
Ons span se naam is "Die Swart Pot", net soos Oupa se ou gietysterpot.\
Vroeg die oggend pak ons die hout, die pot en al die bestanddele in die bakkie.\
Oupa maak 'n vuur, en ek sny die uie, wortels en aartappels.\
Hy braai eers die vleis in 'n bietjie olie totdat dit bruin is.\
Dan pak ons die groente in lae bo-op die vleis.\
"Moet nooit in 'n potjie roer nie," waarsku Oupa, "anders word dit pap."\
Die potjie prut stadig vir drie uur oor die kole.\
Die lekker reuk trek oor die hele veld, en mense kom kyk wat ons kook.\
Teen twaalfuur proe die beoordelaars elke span se kos.\
Hulle knik hul koppe en skryf punte op 'n kaart.\
Ons wen nie die eerste prys nie, maar ons kry 'n sertifikaat vir die beste reuk!\
Oupa glimlag trots en druk my styf vas.\
"Volgende jaar kom ons terug," sê hy.

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Waar word die kompetisie gehou?** In die skoolsaal. / **Op die rugbyveld. ✓** / By die kerk. / In Oupa se tuin. / Op die strand.
2. **Wat is die span se naam?** "Die Groot Pot". / "Oupa se Kos". / **"Die Swart Pot". ✓** / "Die Potjiekoppe". / "Die Kole".
3. **Waarom mag 'n mens nooit in 'n potjie roer nie?** Dit maak die pot vuil. / **Anders word dit pap. ✓** / Die vuur gaan dood. / Die beoordelaars sien dit. / Dit brand aan.
4. **Hoe lank prut die potjie?** Een uur. / Twee uur. / Vier uur. / **Drie uur. ✓** / Vyf uur.
5. **(Dinkvraag) Hoe voel Oupa aan die einde van die dag?** Teleurgesteld. / Kwaad. / Moeg en honger. / Verveeld. / **Trots en gelukkig. ✓**

### Woordherkenning (10)

kompetisie, rugbyveld, gietysterpot, bestanddele, aartappels, groente, waarsku, beoordelaars, sertifikaat, volgende

### Grammatika: verkleinwoorde (5)

*Gee die verkleinwoord – Bv. hond – hondjie*

1. pot – **potjie**
2. ui – **uitjie**
3. kaart – **kaartjie**
4. vuur – **vuurtjie**
5. veld – **veldjie**

### Woordeskat (5)

1. Sy het die \_\_\_\_\_\_ gewen. kompas / **kompetisie ✓** / komitee / kompliment
2. Meel is 'n \_\_\_\_\_\_ van brood. **bestanddeel ✓** / bestanddele / bestelling / bestuurder
3. Die sop \_\_\_\_\_\_ saggies op die stoof. put / pret / **prut ✓** / prys
4. Die \_\_\_\_\_\_ proe al die koeke. beoordeling / beoefenaars / bewaarders / **beoordelaars ✓**
5. Ek het 'n \_\_\_\_\_\_ vir goeie lees gekry. sertifikate / sirkus / seremonie / **sertifikaat ✓**

## Level 3 – Afrikaans 7: Waar kom die krag vandaan?

Topic: How things work. About 157 words, 16 sentences.

### Woordkaarte (6)

**beurtkrag** – Wanneer die krag vir 'n paar uur afgesny word omdat daar nie genoeg elektrisiteit vir almal is nie. Bv. Tydens beurtkrag kook ons op die gasstoof. Engels: "load shedding".

**elektrisiteit** – Die krag wat ligte, yskaste en ander toestelle laat werk. Bv. Die televisie werk met elektrisiteit. Engels: "electricity".

**kragstasie** – 'n Groot gebou waar elektrisiteit gemaak word. Bv. Die kragstasie het hoë skoorstene. Een kragstasie, baie kragstasies. Engels: "power station".

**stoom** – Die warm, wit wolkie wat opstyg wanneer water kook. Bv. Stoom kom uit die ketel. Engels: "steam".

**kragopwekker** – 'n Masjien wat elektrisiteit maak. Bv. Die winkel gebruik 'n kragopwekker tydens beurtkrag. Een kragopwekker, baie kragopwekkers. Engels: "generator".

**sonpaneel** – 'n Plat paneel wat elektrisiteit uit sonlig maak. Bv. Ons skool het sonpanele op die dak. Een sonpaneel, baie sonpanele. Engels: "solar panel".

*Extra cards for more support:* steenkool, kragmas

### Die leesstuk

Een aand is daar skielik beurtkrag, en die hele huis word donker.\
Terwyl hulle by kerslig sit, vra Lara vir Ma waar elektrisiteit vandaan kom.\
Ma verduidelik dat die meeste van ons elektrisiteit by kragstasies gemaak word.\
Daar word steenkool gebrand om water baie warm te maak.\
Die water word stoom, en die stoom draai groot wiele wat turbines genoem word.\
Die turbines draai 'n kragopwekker, wat elektrisiteit maak.\
Dan reis die elektrisiteit deur dik drade op hoë kragmaste.\
Dit kom oor honderde kilometer tot by ons dorp.\
Van daar af loop dit deur kleiner drade tot by elke huis.\
"Elektrisiteit is baie gevaarlik," waarsku Ma.\
"Speel nooit naby kragdrade of met kragproppe nie."\
Ma vertel ook dat party mense sonpanele op hul dakke het.\
Sonpanele maak elektrisiteit uit sonlig, en dit is beter vir die aarde.\
Net toe gaan al die ligte weer aan.\
Selfs die yskas begin weer saggies brom.\
"Hoera!" roep Lara, en sy blaas die kers uit.

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Wat gebeur skielik een aand?** Dit begin reën. / **Daar is beurtkrag, en die huis word donker. ✓** / Die yskas breek. / Die kers val om. / Pa kom huis toe.
2. **Wat word by die kragstasie gebrand?** Hout. / Olie. / **Steenkool. ✓** / Papier. / Gras.
3. **Wat draai die turbines?** Die wind. / **Stoom. ✓** / Mense. / Water uit die rivier. / Diere.
4. **Waarvoor waarsku Ma?** Om nie die kers aan te steek nie. / Om nie laat te slaap nie. / Om nie die yskas oop te maak nie. / **Om nooit naby kragdrade of met kragproppe te speel nie. ✓** / Om nie buite te gaan nie.
5. **(Dinkvraag) Waarom is sonpanele beter vir die aarde?** Hulle is mooi. / Hulle is goedkoop. / Hulle werk net snags. / **Hulle gebruik sonlig in plaas daarvan om steenkool te brand. ✓** / Hulle maak die huis warm.

### Woordherkenning (10)

beurtkrag, kerslig, elektrisiteit, kragstasies, steenkool, stoom, turbines, kragopwekker, gevaarlik, sonpanele

### Grammatika: meervoude (5)

*Gee die meervoud – Bv. hond – honde*

1. draad – **drade**
2. mas – **maste**
3. paneel – **panele**
4. stasie – **stasies**
5. lig – **ligte**

### Woordeskat (5)

1. Tydens \_\_\_\_\_\_ sit ons by kerslig. beurt / **beurtkrag ✓** / beursie / beurtkrae
2. Die \_\_\_\_\_\_ uit die ketel maak die venster nat. storm / stoel / **stoom ✓** / stoot
3. Die winkel gebruik 'n \_\_\_\_\_\_ as die krag af is. **kragopwekker ✓** / kragstasie / kragdraad / kragprop
4. Ons skool het \_\_\_\_\_\_ op die dak. sonpaneel / sonbrille / sonblomme / **sonpanele ✓**
5. Die yskas werk met \_\_\_\_\_\_. elektries / elektrisiën / **elektrisiteit ✓** / elastiek

## Level 3 – Afrikaans 8: Die hart is 'n pomp

Topic: My body and health. About 157 words, 15 sentences.

### Woordkaarte (6)

**spier** – 'n Deel van jou liggaam wat trek en ontspan sodat jy kan beweeg. Bv. Swem maak jou spiere sterk. Een spier, baie spiere. Engels: "muscle".

**vuis** – Jou hand wanneer jy al jou vingers styf toeknyp. Bv. Die bokser maak 'n vuis. Een vuis, baie vuiste. Engels: "fist".

**bloed** – Die rooi vloeistof wat deur jou liggaam vloei. Bv. Daar is 'n bietjie bloed op sy knie. Engels: "blood".

**suurstof** – 'n Gas in die lug wat alle mense en diere nodig het om te lewe. Bv. Plante gee suurstof af. Engels: "oxygen".

**polsslag** – Die klop van jou hart wat jy aan jou pols of nek kan voel. Bv. Die dokter tel my polsslag. Engels: "pulse".

**oefening** – Wanneer jy jou liggaam beweeg om fiks en gesond te bly. Bv. Swem is goeie oefening. Een oefening, baie oefeninge. Engels: "exercise".

*Extra cards for more support:* pols, pomp

### Die leesstuk

In die LO-klas leer Meneer Dlamini die kinders oor die hart.\
"Die hart is 'n spier, omtrent so groot soos jou vuis," sê hy.\
Dit pomp bloed deur jou hele liggaam, dag en nag, sonder om te rus.\
Die bloed dra suurstof en kos na elke deel van jou lyf.\
Meneer wys hulle hoe om hul polsslag aan hul pols te voel.\
Elke klop wat hulle voel, is een keer wat die hart pomp.\
Die kinders tel hul polsslag vir een minuut terwyl hulle stil sit.\
Sipho s'n klop tagtig keer per minuut.\
Dan laat Meneer hulle vir twee minute op die plek hardloop.\
Toe hulle weer tel, klop Sipho se hart meer as honderd keer!\
"Wanneer jy oefen, het jou spiere meer suurstof nodig," verduidelik Meneer.\
"Daarom pomp jou hart vinniger."\
Hy vertel dat oefening die hart sterker maak, net soos enige ander spier.\
'n Sterk hart hoef nie so hard te werk nie.\
Sipho besluit om elke dag na skool te gaan fietsry.

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Hoe groot is die hart omtrent?** So groot soos 'n bal. / **So groot soos jou vuis. ✓** / So groot soos 'n boek. / So groot soos jou kop. / So groot soos 'n ertjie.
2. **Wat dra die bloed na elke deel van jou lyf?** Water en sout. / **Suurstof en kos. ✓** / Lug en lig. / Suiker en melk. / Hitte en koue.
3. **Hoeveel keer klop Sipho se hart terwyl hy stil sit?** Sestig keer. / Veertig keer. / Honderd keer. / **Tagtig keer. ✓** / Twintig keer.
4. **Waarom pomp die hart vinniger wanneer jy oefen?** Omdat jy bang is. / Omdat jy warm is. / **Omdat jou spiere meer suurstof nodig het. ✓** / Omdat jy moeg is. / Omdat jy honger is.
5. **(Dinkvraag) Waarom besluit Sipho om elke dag te gaan fietsry?** Hy het 'n nuwe fiets gekry. / Sy maat ry ook. / Meneer het hom gestraf. / Hy wil vinniger by die huis kom. / **Hy wil sy hart sterker maak. ✓**

### Woordherkenning (10)

hart, spier, omtrent, liggaam, suurstof, polsslag, tagtig, honderd, oefening, sterker

### Grammatika: trappe van vergelyking (5)

*Voltooi die trappe van vergelyking – Bv. klein – kleiner – kleinste*

1. sterk – **sterker** – **sterkste**
2. hard – **harder** – **hardste**
3. gesond – **gesonder** – **gesondste**
4. stil – **stiller** – **stilste**
5. lank – **langer** – **langste**

### Woordeskat (5)

1. Die hart is 'n sterk \_\_\_\_\_\_. **spier ✓** / spieël / spiere / spyker
2. Die bokser knyp sy hand in 'n \_\_\_\_\_\_. vis / **vuis ✓** / vuil / vuiste
3. Ons het \_\_\_\_\_\_ nodig om asem te haal. suurlemoen / suiker / **suurstof ✓** / suurmelk
4. Die verpleegster tel my \_\_\_\_\_\_. polisie / poskantoor / polsband / **polsslag ✓**
5. Swem is goeie \_\_\_\_\_\_ vir jou hele liggaam. oefen / oewer / **oefening ✓** / oefenaar

## Level 3 – Afrikaans 9: Die reën kom na die Karoo

Topic: Nature and outdoors. About 164 words, 14 sentences.

### Woordkaarte (6)

**behoorlik** – Soos dit moet wees; genoeg. Bv. Was jou hande behoorlik voor ete. Engels: "properly".

**bekommerd** – Wanneer jy bang is dat iets sleg gaan gebeur. Bv. Ma was bekommerd toe ek laat was. Engels: "worried".

**voer** – Kos vir diere. Bv. Die boer gee die koeie voer. Engels: "feed".

**saampak** – Om bymekaar te kom en al hoe meer te word. Bv. Die mense pak by die hek saam. Dit pak saam, dit het saamgepak. Engels: "gather / build up".

**leivoor** – 'n Smal sloot waardeur water loop. Bv. Die water loop in die leivoor na die tuin. Een leivoor, baie leivore. Engels: "irrigation ditch".

**dankbaar** – Wanneer jy bly is oor iets wat jy gekry het en dankie wil sê. Bv. Ek is dankbaar vir my familie. Engels: "grateful".

*Extra cards for more support:* sinkdak, wolkloos

### Die leesstuk

Dit het al meer as 'n jaar nie behoorlik op Oom Piet se plaas gereën nie.\
Die dam is leeg, en die veld is droog en bruin.\
Elke oggend kyk Oom Piet bekommerd na die wolklose lug.\
Die skape word maer, en hy moet duur voer vir hulle koop.\
Een warm middag begin groot, donker wolke in die weste saampak.\
'n Koel wind waai, en die reuk van nat grond trek oor die veld.\
Toe begin die eerste groot druppels op die sinkdak kletter.\
Binne minute reën dit so hard dat die leivore oorloop.\
Die kinders hardloop buite en dans in die reën.\
Selfs Oom Piet staan met sy hoed in sy hand en laat die water oor sy gesig loop.\
Die volgende oggend het die dam al halfvol geloop.\
'n Week later is die veld skielik vol groen gras en helder blomme.\
Paddas kwaak in die dam, en die voëls sing harder as ooit.\
"Reën is die grootste geskenk vir 'n boer," sê Oom Piet dankbaar.

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Hoe lank het dit nie behoorlik gereën nie?** 'n Week. / 'n Maand. / **Meer as 'n jaar. ✓** / Twee dae. / Tien jaar.
2. **Wat moet Oom Piet vir die skape koop?** Water. / Medisyne. / **Voer. ✓** / Komberse. / Sout.
3. **Waar pak die wolke saam?** **In die weste. ✓** / In die ooste. / In die noorde. / In die suide. / Oor die dam.
4. **Hoe lyk die veld 'n week later?** Nog steeds droog en bruin. / Vol sneeu. / **Vol groen gras en helder blomme. ✓** / Onder water. / Vol skape.
5. **(Dinkvraag) Waarom staan Oom Piet met sy hoed in sy hand in die reën?** Hy het sy hoed verloor. / Dit is baie warm. / Hy wil sy hoed was. / Hy is kwaad. / **Hy is so bly en dankbaar oor die reën. ✓**

### Woordherkenning (10)

behoorlik, bekommerd, wolklose, voer, weste, druppels, sinkdak, leivore, halfvol, dankbaar

### Grammatika: teenoorgesteldes (5)

*Gee die teenoorgestelde – Bv. groot – klein*

1. maer – **vet**
2. duur – **goedkoop**
3. weste – **ooste**
4. dankbaar – **ondankbaar**
5. bekommerd – **gerus** *(accept: kalm)*

### Woordeskat (5)

1. Ma is \_\_\_\_\_\_ omdat die hond weg is. bekom / beroemd / **bekommerd ✓** / bekeerd
2. Die boer koop \_\_\_\_\_\_ vir sy beeste. **voer ✓** / vuur / voet / vier
3. Die water loop deur die \_\_\_\_\_\_ na die boord. leeu / lewer / lêer / **leivoor ✓**
4. Ek is \_\_\_\_\_\_ vir my ouma se hulp. dank / **dankbaar ✓** / dankie / danksegging
5. Donker wolke begin in die weste \_\_\_\_\_\_. saamsing / saamgaan / **saampak ✓** / saampakke

## Level 3 – Afrikaans 10: Die skubdier

Topic: Animals. About 160 words, 16 sentences.

### Woordkaarte (6)

**skub** – 'n Klein, harde plaatjie op die vel van 'n vis, slang of skubdier. Bv. Die vis se skubbe blink in die son. Een skub, baie skubbe. Engels: "scale".

**bedek** – Om iets toe te maak of daaroor te sit. Bv. Sneeu bedek die berge. Ek bedek, ek het bedek. Engels: "cover".

**taai** – Klewerig, sodat dinge daaraan vasplak. Bv. My vingers is taai van die heuning. Engels: "sticky".

**termiet** – 'n Klein insek wat in groot neste in die grond woon en hout eet. Bv. Termiete het die ou deur opgevreet. Een termiet, baie termiete. Engels: "termite".

**onwettig** – Teen die wet; iets wat nie toegelaat word nie. Bv. Dit is onwettig om te vinnig te ry. Teenoorgestelde: wettig. Engels: "illegal".

**red** – Om iemand of iets uit gevaar te help. Bv. Die brandweerman red die kat uit die boom. Ek red, ek het gered. Engels: "save / rescue".

*Extra cards for more support:* skaam, miershoop

### Die leesstuk

Het jy al ooit van 'n skubdier gehoor?\
Dit is een van die vreemdste diere in Afrika.\
Sy hele lyf, behalwe sy maag en gesig, is met harde skubbe bedek.\
Die skubbe is van dieselfde stof as jou naels gemaak.\
As die skubdier bang word, rol hy homself op soos 'n bal.\
Selfs 'n leeu kan hom dan nie seermaak nie!\
Skubdiere het geen tande nie.\
In plaas daarvan het hulle 'n lang, taai tong wat amper so lank soos hul lyf kan wees.\
Hul skerp kloue help hulle om miershope oop te grawe.\
Dan lek hulle die miere en termiete met hul tong op.\
Een skubdier kan in een jaar miljoene insekte eet.\
Skubdiere is baie skaam en kom meestal net snags uit.\
'n Ma-skubdier dra haar kleintjie op haar stert rond.\
Ongelukkig is hulle in gevaar, want mense jag hulle onwettig vir hul skubbe.\
Natuurbewaarders werk hard om hierdie spesiale diere te beskerm.\
As ons meer van hulle weet, kan ons help om hulle te red.

### Begrip (5 vrae, ✓ = reg)

1. **Waarvan is die skubdier se skubbe gemaak?** Van been. / **Van dieselfde stof as jou naels. ✓** / Van klip. / Van hout. / Van plastiek.
2. **Wat doen 'n skubdier as hy bang word?** Hy hardloop weg. / Hy klim in 'n boom. / **Hy rol homself op soos 'n bal. ✓** / Hy byt. / Hy skree hard.
3. **Wat eet skubdiere?** Blare. / Vis. / Vrugte. / **Miere en termiete. ✓** / Muise.
4. **Hoe dra 'n ma-skubdier haar kleintjie?** In haar bek. / In 'n sak. / Op haar kop. / Onder haar maag. / **Op haar stert. ✓**
5. **(Dinkvraag) Waarom is dit belangrik dat ons meer oor skubdiere leer?** **Sodat ons kan help om hulle te beskerm en te red. ✓** / Sodat ons hulle kan vang. / Sodat ons hulle as troeteldiere kan hou. / Omdat hulle gevaarlik is. / Omdat hulle ons kos eet.

### Woordherkenning (10)

skubdier, vreemdste, skubbe, naels, tande, termiete, miershope, onwettig, natuurbewaarders, beskerm

### Grammatika: teenoorgesteldes met on- (5)

*Gee die teenoorgestelde met on- – Bv. gesond – ongesond*

1. wettig – **onwettig**
2. bekend – **onbekend**
3. moontlik – **onmoontlik**
4. gelukkig – **ongelukkig**
5. vriendelik – **onvriendelik**

### Woordeskat (5)

1. Die vis se \_\_\_\_\_\_ blink in die son. **skubbe ✓** / skape / skuite / skobbe
2. Sneeu \_\_\_\_\_\_ die hoë berge. bederf / **bedek ✓** / bedel / beddens
3. My vingers is \_\_\_\_\_\_ van die stroop. taal / tas / **taai ✓** / tou
4. Dit is \_\_\_\_\_\_ om skubdiere te jag. wettig / onwetend / wetenskap / **onwettig ✓**
5. Die brandweerman \_\_\_\_\_\_ die kat uit die boom. rede / ry / rek / **red ✓**

## Level 3 – English: Mia's Lemonade Stand

About 159 words, 13 sentences, one sentence per line.

### Word cards (6)

**earn** – To get money for work you have done. e.g. Tom earns money by washing his neighbour's car. I earn, I earned. Afrikaans: "verdien".

**squeeze** – To press something hard with your hands to get the juice or water out. e.g. Squeeze the orange to make fresh juice. I squeeze, I squeezed. Afrikaans: "uitdruk".

**mixture** – Different things that have been mixed together. e.g. Pour the cake mixture into the pan. One mixture, many mixtures. Afrikaans: "mengsel".

**sour** – A sharp taste, like a lemon or vinegar. e.g. The green apple was so sour that I pulled a face. Opposite: sweet. Afrikaans: "suur".

**disappointed** – Sad because something did not happen the way you hoped. e.g. Ben was disappointed when the rain spoiled the picnic. Afrikaans: "teleurgesteld".

**thirsty** – When your body needs something to drink. e.g. After running around, the dog was very thirsty. Afrikaans: "dors".

*Extra cards for more support:* ripe, shady

### The passage

It was a hot Saturday, and Mia wanted to earn some pocket money.\
She decided to open a lemonade stand in front of her house.\
First, she picked ten ripe lemons from the tree in the garden.\
Her dad helped her squeeze the lemons into a big glass jug.\
Then she added cold water, sugar and lots of ice.\
Mia stirred the mixture carefully and tasted it with a spoon.\
"It is a little sour," she said, so she added a bit more sugar.\
Next, she painted a bright yellow sign that said "Fresh Lemonade – R5".\
She set up a small table and two chairs under a shady tree.\
At first nobody stopped, and Mia began to feel disappointed.\
Then a group of thirsty children came home from soccer practice.\
They each bought a cup, and soon the jug was completely empty.\
Mia counted her coins and smiled, because she had earned enough to buy a new book.

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **Why did Mia open a lemonade stand?** She wanted to make new friends / help her dad / **earn some pocket money ✓** / use up the sugar / stay out of the sun.
2. **How many lemons did Mia pick?** She picked two / five / twelve / **ten ✓** / twenty lemons.
3. **Why did Mia add more sugar?** The lemonade was a little too warm / **sour ✓** / sweet / watery / salty.
4. **What colour was Mia's sign?** The sign was bright red / blue / **yellow ✓** / green / pink.
5. **Where were the thirsty children coming from?** They were coming home from school / swimming lessons / the park / **soccer practice ✓** / church.

### Word recognition (10)

earn, squeeze, mixture, sour, disappointed, thirsty, lemonade, carefully, ripe, completely

1. plant – **planted**
2. bake – **baked**
3. stop – **stopped**
4. carry – **carried**
5. buy – **bought**

## Level 3 – English 2: Up Table Mountain

Topic: Nature and outdoors. About 163 words, 15 sentences.

### Word cards (6)

**hike** – A long walk in nature, often up hills or mountains. e.g. We went on a hike in the Drakensberg. I hike, I hiked. Afrikaans: "voetslaan".

**steep** – Going up or down very sharply. e.g. The road up the hill is very steep. Afrikaans: "steil".

**gorge** – A deep, narrow valley with steep, rocky sides. e.g. A river flows through the gorge. One gorge, many gorges. Afrikaans: "kloof".

**admire** – To look at something and think it is beautiful or wonderful. e.g. We admired the sunset. I admire, I admired. Afrikaans: "bewonder".

**related** – Part of the same family. e.g. Lions and house cats are related. Afrikaans: "verwant".

**cable car** – A small cabin that hangs from a strong wire and carries people up and down a mountain. e.g. The cable car swung gently in the wind. Afrikaans: "kabelkar".

*Extra cards for more support:* fynbos, dassie

### The passage

On Saturday, Josh and his mom hiked up Table Mountain in Cape Town.\
They started early, before the sun became too hot.\
The path through Platteklip Gorge was steep and rocky.\
Along the way, they saw colourful fynbos plants and a few proteas.\
Mom explained that fynbos grows only in this part of the world.\
After two hours, Josh's legs felt as heavy as bricks.\
They stopped often to drink water and admire the view.\
Far below them, the city looked like a toy town.\
Near the top, a furry dassie was sunning itself on a rock.\
"It looks like a big guinea pig," laughed Josh.\
Mom told him that dassies are actually related to elephants!\
At last they reached the flat top of the mountain.\
Josh could see Robben Island and the blue ocean all around.\
Then a cold wind blew in, and a white cloud covered the mountain like a tablecloth.\
They took the cable car down, tired but very proud.

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **Why did they start early?** To see the sunrise. / **Before the sun became too hot. ✓** / To catch the cable car. / Because Mom had to work. / To beat the crowds.
2. **Where does fynbos grow?** All over the world. / In deserts. / In rivers. / **Only in this part of the world. ✓** / Only in gardens.
3. **What did the dassie look like to Josh?** **A big guinea pig. ✓** / A rabbit. / A cat. / A rat. / A puppy.
4. **What covered the mountain like a tablecloth?** Snow. / **A white cloud. ✓** / Smoke. / Dust. / Rain.
5. **(Thinking) Why were Josh and his mom proud?** They took lots of photos. / They saw a dassie. / They won a race. / They rode the cable car. / **They had climbed all the way to the top. ✓**

### Word recognition (10)

2. see – **saw**
3. take – **took**
4. blow – **blew**
5. tell – **told**

### Vocabulary (5)

2. We went on a long \_\_\_\_\_\_ in the mountains. hook / hive / **hike ✓** / hikes
3. A river runs through the deep \_\_\_\_\_\_. George / gauge / gorgeous / **gorge ✓**
4. We stopped to \_\_\_\_\_\_ the beautiful view. **admire ✓** / admit / admiral / admired

## Level 3 – English 3: The Warm Fridge

Topic: How things work. About 166 words, 15 sentences.

### Word cards (6)

**melt** – To change from solid to liquid when it gets warm. e.g. The snowman melted in the sun. It melts, it melted. Afrikaans: "smelt".

**liquid** – Something that flows, like water or milk. e.g. Juice is a liquid. One liquid, many liquids. Afrikaans: "vloeistof".

**soak up** – To take in liquid or heat, like a sponge takes in water. e.g. The towel soaked up the spilt milk. Afrikaans: "opsuig".

**compressor** – The pump in a fridge that pushes a special liquid through its pipes. e.g. The compressor makes a soft humming sound. Afrikaans: "kompressor".

**escape** – To get out of a place. e.g. The bird escaped from its cage. It escapes, it escaped. Afrikaans: "ontsnap".

**replace** – To put something new in the place of something old or broken. e.g. Mom replaced the broken light bulb. I replace, I replaced. Afrikaans: "vervang".

*Extra cards for more support:* hum, science

### The passage

One hot morning, Ayanda opened the fridge and found that it was warm inside.\
The milk smelt sour, and the ice cream had melted into a sticky puddle.\
Dad phoned a repairman named Mr Botha.\
While he worked, Ayanda asked him how a fridge keeps food cold.\
"A fridge doesn't make cold," said Mr Botha.\
"Instead, it takes heat out of the inside and pushes it outside."\
He showed her thin pipes at the back of the fridge.\
A special liquid flows through these pipes and soaks up the heat from the food.\
A pump, called a compressor, then pushes the warm liquid to the back.\
There, the heat escapes into the kitchen air.\
"That is why the back of a fridge always feels warm," he explained.\
Mr Botha found that the compressor was broken and replaced it.\
Soon the fridge was humming again, and the inside grew cold.\
Ayanda put her hand near the back and felt the warm air.\
"Science is everywhere!" she said.

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **What had happened to the ice cream?** It was frozen hard. / **It had melted into a sticky puddle. ✓** / It was gone. / It had turned green. / It was on the floor.
2. **Who did Dad phone?** Gran. / A plumber. / Ayanda's teacher. / **A repairman named Mr Botha. ✓** / The shop.
3. **According to Mr Botha, what does a fridge do?** It makes cold air. / It uses ice. / **It takes heat out of the inside and pushes it outside. ✓** / It blows wind. / It freezes the pipes.
4. **What was broken?** The door. / The light. / The plug. / The shelves. / **The compressor. ✓**
5. **(Thinking) Why does the back of a fridge feel warm?** **Heat from inside the fridge escapes there. ✓** / The motor is on fire. / The sun shines on it. / It stands next to the stove. / It is broken.

### Word recognition (10)

fridge, sour, melted, sticky, repairman, liquid, compressor, escapes, replaced, science

### Grammar: compound words (5)

*Join the two words – e.g. sun + flower = sunflower*

1. repair + man – **repairman**
2. tea + spoon – **teaspoon**
3. foot + ball – **football**
4. rain + bow – **rainbow**
5. cup + board – **cupboard**

### Vocabulary (5)

1. The chocolate \_\_\_\_\_\_ in my hot pocket. melt / **melted ✓** / metal / malted
2. Juice is a \_\_\_\_\_\_. liquids / limit / lid / **liquid ✓**
3. The sponge will \_\_\_\_\_\_ up the water. **soak ✓** / sock / soap / soaks
4. The dog tried to \_\_\_\_\_\_ through the hole in the fence. escalate / **escape ✓** / scrape / escapes
5. We need to \_\_\_\_\_\_ the broken window. replay / relax / replaced / **replace ✓**

## Level 3 – English 4: Why We Need Water

Topic: My body and health. About 162 words, 14 sentences.

### Word cards (6)

**headache** – A pain in your head. e.g. Loud music gives me a headache. One headache, many headaches. Afrikaans: "hoofpyn".

**dizzy** – Feeling as if everything is spinning around you. e.g. I felt dizzy after the merry-go-round. Afrikaans: "duiselig".

**sweat** – Salty water that comes out of your skin when you are hot. e.g. Sweat dripped down his face. I sweat, I sweated. Afrikaans: "sweet".

**dehydrated** – When your body does not have enough water. e.g. Drink water so that you don't get dehydrated. Afrikaans: "uitgedroog".

**fuel** – Something like petrol that gives a machine the power to work. e.g. The car needs fuel to go. Afrikaans: "brandstof".

**contain** – To have something inside. e.g. This box contains toys. It contains, it contained. Afrikaans: "bevat".

*Extra cards for more support:* properly, energy

### The passage

On the first day of the school holidays, Ben played soccer all morning in the sun.\
By lunchtime, he had a headache and felt dizzy and tired.\
"When did you last drink some water?" asked his gran.\
Ben couldn't remember, so Gran poured him a big glass.\
She explained that more than half of the human body is made of water.\
Our bodies lose water all day long when we sweat, breathe and go to the toilet.\
If we don't drink enough, we become dehydrated.\
Then our brains can't work properly, and we feel weak.\
"Your body is like a car that needs fuel," said Gran.\
"Water is the fuel that keeps everything running smoothly."\
Ben learnt that it's best to drink water often, even before he feels thirsty.\
Fruit like watermelon and oranges also contain lots of water.\
That afternoon, Ben filled a bottle and took it everywhere with him.\
His headache soon disappeared, and he felt full of energy again.

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **How did Ben feel at lunchtime?** Hungry and happy. / **He had a headache and felt dizzy and tired. ✓** / Cold and wet. / Full of energy. / Excited.
2. **How much of the human body is made of water?** A quarter. / All of it. / A little bit. / **More than half. ✓** / None of it.
3. **What happens if we don't drink enough water?** We grow taller. / We get stronger. / **We become dehydrated. ✓** / We get hungry. / We feel cold.
4. **What does Gran compare the body to?** A plant. / A sponge. / A phone. / A house. / **A car that needs fuel. ✓**
5. **(Thinking) Why did Ben take a water bottle everywhere?** **So he could drink often and not get dehydrated again. ✓** / Because he liked the bottle. / To share with friends. / Because Gran made him. / To water the plants.

### Word recognition (10)

holidays, headache, dizzy, remember, explained, sweat, dehydrated, properly, watermelon, energy

2. did not – **didn't**
3. do not – **don't**
4. he is – **he's**
5. I am – **I'm**

3. After running, my shirt was wet with \_\_\_\_\_\_. **sweat ✓** / sweet / sweater / swept
4. The car stopped because it ran out of \_\_\_\_\_\_. fool / full / fuels / **fuel ✓**
5. This bottle \_\_\_\_\_\_ fresh orange juice. contain / **contains ✓** / container / continues

## Level 3 – English 5: The Toughest Little Animal

Topic: Animals. About 161 words, 12 sentences.

### Word cards (6)

**brave** – Not afraid to do something dangerous or difficult. e.g. The brave firefighter saved the cat. Afrikaans: "dapper".

**loose** – Not tight. e.g. My tooth is loose. Opposite: tight. Afrikaans: "los".

**twist** – To turn something around. e.g. Twist the lid to open the jar. I twist, I twisted. Afrikaans: "draai".

**venomous** – Able to hurt with poison through a bite or sting. e.g. The puff adder is a venomous snake. Afrikaans: "giftig".

**claw** – A sharp, curved nail on an animal's foot. e.g. The cat scratched me with its claws. One claw, many claws. Afrikaans: "klou".

**tough** – Very strong and hard to hurt or break. e.g. These boots are tough enough for hiking. Afrikaans: "taai".

*Extra cards for more support:* survive, hive

### The passage

The honey badger may be small, but it is one of the bravest animals in Africa.\
It is about the size of a medium dog, with black fur and a white stripe on its back.\
Its skin is thick and loose, like a coat that is a size too big.\
If a lion grabs it, the honey badger can twist around inside its skin and bite back!\
Honey badgers eat almost anything, from insects and frogs to snakes and scorpions.\
Some can even survive a bite from a venomous snake.\
As their name suggests, they love honey and the baby bees inside a hive.\
They use their long, sharp claws to tear the hive open.\
The angry bees sting them, but their thick skin protects them.\
Honey badgers are also very clever.\
At one wildlife centre, a honey badger learnt to open gates and stack stones to climb out of its enclosure.\
No wonder people call it the toughest animal in the world!

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **What colour is the stripe on its back?** Black. / **White. ✓** / Brown. / Grey. / Yellow.
2. **What can a honey badger do if a lion grabs it?** Run away fast. / Play dead. / **Twist around inside its skin and bite back. ✓** / Climb a tree. / Roar loudly.
3. **How does it open a beehive?** With its teeth. / With its tail. / With stones. / **With its long, sharp claws. ✓** / With smoke.
4. **Why don't the bee stings hurt it much?** It is very fast. / Bees like it. / It eats at night. / It wears a coat. / **Its thick skin protects it. ✓**
5. **(Thinking) Why do people call it the toughest animal?** **It is brave, clever and hard to hurt. ✓** / It is the biggest animal. / It can fly. / It is the fastest runner. / It lives the longest.

### Word recognition (10)

badger, bravest, stripe, loose, twist, scorpions, venomous, claws, protects, toughest

2. tough – **toughest**
3. small – **smallest**
4. angry – **angriest**
5. loose – **loosest**

4. The puff adder is a \_\_\_\_\_\_ snake. venom / generous / nervous / **venomous ✓**

## Level 3 – English 6: Thandi Learns to Surf

Topic: Sport and games. About 166 words, 13 sentences.

### Word cards (6)

**instructor** – A person who teaches a skill like swimming, driving or surfing. e.g. My swimming instructor is very patient. One instructor, many instructors. Afrikaans: "instrukteur".

**wetsuit** – A tight rubber suit that keeps you warm in cold water. e.g. Divers wear wetsuits. One wetsuit, many wetsuits. Afrikaans: "duikpak".

**paddle** – To move through water using your arms or an oar. e.g. We paddled the canoe across the dam. I paddle, I paddled. Afrikaans: "roei".

**balance** – Being steady so that you don't fall over. e.g. It's hard to keep your balance on one leg. Afrikaans: "balans".

**shallow** – Not deep. e.g. The little children play in the shallow end of the pool. Opposite: deep. Afrikaans: "vlak".

**wobble** – To move unsteadily from side to side. e.g. The jelly wobbled on the plate. I wobble, I wobbled. Afrikaans: "wiebel".

*Extra cards for more support:* surfboard, ache

### The passage

During the December holidays, Thandi had her first surfing lesson at Muizenberg Beach.\
Her instructor, Kyle, gave her a thick wetsuit and a big foam surfboard.\
He drew a surfboard shape in the sand and showed her how to lie on it.\
Thandi learnt to paddle with her arms and then jump up quickly onto her feet.\
She had to keep her knees bent and her arms out for balance.\
After ten practice runs, they carried the board into the shallow water.\
Kyle held the back of the board while they waited for a small wave.\
"Paddle, paddle, paddle!" he shouted as the wave arrived.\
Thandi paddled hard and tried to stand up, but she wobbled and fell off with a splash.\
She tried again and again, swallowing plenty of salty water.\
On her ninth try, she finally stood up and rode the wave all the way to the beach.\
Thandi grinned from ear to ear, even though her arms were aching.\
"Can I come back tomorrow?" she asked.

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **Where did Thandi have her first surfing lesson?** In Durban. / **At Muizenberg Beach. ✓** / At Jeffreys Bay. / In Plettenberg Bay. / In a swimming pool.
2. **Where did they practise first?** In the deep water. / In the car park. / **On the sand. ✓** / In the pool. / On a rock.
3. **What did Kyle do while they waited for a wave?** He swam away. / **He held the back of the board. ✓** / He took photos. / He sat on the beach. / He surfed his own wave.
4. **On which try did Thandi finally stand up?** Her second try. / Her fifth try. / Her tenth try. / **Her ninth try. ✓** / Her first try.
5. **(Thinking) What does Thandi's question at the end tell us?** She was scared. / She was bored. / She wanted to go home. / She was hungry. / **She loved surfing and wants to do it again. ✓**

### Word recognition (10)

2. swim – **swimming**
3. ride – **riding**
4. try – **trying**
5. surf – **surfing**

4. The jelly \_\_\_\_\_\_ on the plate. wobble / bubbled / warbled / **wobbled ✓**

## Level 3 – English 7: Life on the Space Station

Topic: Space and stars. About 166 words, 13 sentences.

### Word cards (6)

**circle** – To move around something in a ring. e.g. The eagle circled above the field. It circles, it circled. Afrikaans: "omsirkel".

**gravity** – The force that pulls things down towards the Earth. e.g. Gravity makes an apple fall from a tree. Afrikaans: "swaartekrag".

**precious** – Very valuable and important. e.g. Clean water is precious. Afrikaans: "kosbaar".

**exercise** – Moving your body to keep fit and healthy. e.g. Swimming is good exercise. Afrikaans: "oefening".

**recycle** – To use something again, or turn it into something new. e.g. We recycle old paper and tins. I recycle, I recycled. Afrikaans: "herwin".

**atmosphere** – The layer of air around the Earth. e.g. Clouds float in the atmosphere. Afrikaans: "atmosfeer".

*Extra cards for more support:* experiment, border

### The passage

High above the Earth, a huge spacecraft called the International Space Station circles our planet.\
It travels so fast that it goes around the whole Earth in about ninety minutes.\
That means the astronauts on board see about sixteen sunrises every day!\
Up to seven astronauts from different countries live and work there together.\
Because there is almost no gravity, everything floats.\
The astronauts sleep in sleeping bags tied to the walls so they don't drift around.\
They have to exercise for about two hours every day.\
Without exercise, their muscles and bones would become weak.\
Water is so precious that they even clean and recycle their sweat and urine into drinking water!\
The astronauts also do science experiments that can't be done on Earth.\
Through the windows, they can see storms, cities lit up at night and the thin blue line of our atmosphere.\
Many astronauts say that seeing Earth from space changed how they think about our planet.\
It looks small and beautiful, with no borders between countries.

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

1. **How long does the space station take to go around the Earth?** A whole day. / **About ninety minutes. ✓** / A week. / Ten minutes. / A year.
2. **How many sunrises do the astronauts see every day?** One. / Two. / **About sixteen. ✓** / Six. / Sixty.
3. **Why do the astronauts sleep in bags tied to the walls?** To keep warm. / To stay quiet. / Because there are no beds. / **So that they don't drift around. ✓** / To hide from the light.
4. **Why must the astronauts exercise every day?** To win races. / To keep warm. / **So that their muscles and bones don't become weak. ✓** / To pass the time. / To make friends.
5. **(Thinking) Why do many astronauts think differently about Earth after seeing it from space?** It looks flat. / It is too hot. / They miss the food at home. / They are very tired. / **It looks small and beautiful, with no borders between countries. ✓**

### Word recognition (10)

station, planet, ninety, sunrises, astronauts, gravity, exercise, precious, recycle, atmosphere

### Grammar: compound words (5)

*Join the two words – e.g. sun + flower = sunflower*

1. sun + rise – **sunrise**
2. space + craft – **spacecraft**
3. with + out – **without**
4. every + thing – **everything**
5. day + time – **daytime**

### Vocabulary (5)

2. Clean water is very \_\_\_\_\_\_ in a drought. previous / pressure / precise / **precious ✓**
3. We \_\_\_\_\_\_ our glass bottles every week. bicycle / **recycle ✓** / recite / recipe
4. Clouds float in the Earth's \_\_\_\_\_\_. **atmosphere ✓** / astronaut / asteroid / attic
5. The moon \_\_\_\_\_\_ the Earth about once a month. circus / circle / **circles ✓** / cereals

## Level 3 – English 8: The Message in a Bottle

Topic: Adventure and make-believe. About 165 words, 14 sentences.

### Word cards (6)

**spot** – To notice or see something. e.g. I spotted a zebra behind the trees. I spot, I spotted. Afrikaans: "raaksien".

**cork** – A stopper for a bottle, made from the bark of a tree. e.g. Dad pulled the cork out of the bottle. One cork, many corks. Afrikaans: "kurkprop".

**neat** – Tidy and carefully done. e.g. Your handwriting is very neat. Opposite: messy. Afrikaans: "netjies".

**request** – When you ask for something politely. e.g. The radio played my song request. One request, many requests. Afrikaans: "versoek".

**trace** – To follow a line or path with your finger or a pencil. e.g. Trace the letters on the page. I trace, I traced. Afrikaans: "natrek".

**envelope** – A paper cover for a letter. e.g. Put the card in an envelope. One envelope, many envelopes. Afrikaans: "koevert".

*Extra cards for more support:* journey, marine

### The passage

While walking along the beach with her dog, Lerato spotted something shiny in the seaweed.\
It was an old green bottle with a cork in the top.\
Inside, she could see a rolled-up piece of paper.\
Carefully, she pulled out the cork and shook the paper into her hand.\
The letter was written in neat handwriting and dated ten years ago!\
"My name is Emma, and I am nine years old," it said. "I live in Australia."\
"I threw this bottle into the sea to see how far it would travel."\
At the bottom was an address and a request to write back.\
Lerato raced home to show her parents.\
Together they looked at a map and traced the long journey across the Indian Ocean.\
That evening, Lerato and her parents wrote a letter and posted it to the address.\
Weeks later, a thick envelope with colourful stamps arrived.\
Emma was now nineteen and studying to become a marine scientist.\
Now the two families write to each other every year.

### Comprehension (5 questions, ✓ = correct)

2. **How old was the letter?** One year old. / Five years old. / **Ten years old. ✓** / Twenty years old. / Fifty years old.
3. **Where did Emma live?** In England. / In Brazil. / In India. / **In Australia. ✓** / In Namibia.
4. **What is Emma studying now?** To become a teacher. / To become a pilot. / To become a doctor. / To become a sailor. / **To become a marine scientist. ✓**
5. **(Thinking) How do you think Lerato felt when the envelope arrived?** **Surprised and excited. ✓** / Angry. / Bored. / Scared. / Sad.
